### [10] [Fine-Tuning Qwen3-27B for C-to-Rust Code Translation: A Three-Stage Curriculum of Pretraining, Debugging-Aware SFT, and Task-Specific SFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13681)
*Pu Zhao,Changdi Yang,Yixiao Chen,Yi Gao,Yifan Cao,Haochen Zeng,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段微调课程策略，将Qwen3-27B逐步专业化用于C到Rust的安全翻译任务：先在Rust语料上继续预训练加强惯用语法先验，再在Verus_Training_Data上SFT培养调试和自修复能力，最后在LeetCode配对数据上SFT教授直接语义翻译。使用SACTOR框架（静态度量分析引导、两阶段翻译、FFI端到端测试）评估，报告成功率和惯用性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将C代码翻译成安全、惯用的Rust是长期追求的软件工程目标，可以消除内存安全漏洞并保留遗留系统行为。虽然LLM在此任务上有潜力，但通用预训练模型直接使用时表现不佳，因为缺乏对惯用Rust生成、跨语言语义等价性以及编译/运行时反馈推理修复能力的专门训练。

Method: 采用三阶段微调课程：(1)在Rust中心语料上继续预训练强化惯用语法和标准库使用先验；(2)在microsoft/Verus_Training_Data数据集上SFT以培养Rust代码调试和自修复行为；(3)在LeetCode配对的C/Rust解决方案上SFT教授直接语义翻译。评估使用SACTOR智能体框架，该框架执行结构感知的两阶段（非惯用到惯用）翻译，并通过FFI进行端到端测试。

Result: 报告了成功率、惯用性指标（Clippy lint计数、unsafe代码比例）以及失败模式分析，并将微调后的模型与基线Qwen3-27B及在同一框架下评估的其他LLM进行了比较。

Conclusion: 三阶段微调课程能够逐步专业化通用LLM以胜任C2Rust翻译任务，通过SACTOR框架的系统评估验证了该方法的有效性，为LLM在不同编程语言间语义等价转换提供了可复现的范式。

Abstract: Translating C code into safe, idiomatic Rust is a longstanding software-engineering goal because it can eliminate entire classes of memory-safety vulnerabilities while preserving the functional behavior of legacy systems. Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for this task but typically underperform when applied off-the-shelf, since general-purpose pretraining rarely emphasizes idiomatic Rust generation, cross-language semantic equivalence, or the ability to reason about and repair compiler/runtime feedback. In this report we describe a three-stage fine-tuning curriculum applied to Qwen3-27B that is designed to progressively specialize the model for the C-to-Rust (C2Rust) translation task: (1) continued pretraining on Rust-centric corpora to strengthen the model's prior over idiomatic Rust syntax and standard-library usage; (2) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on the microsoft/Verus_Training_Data dataset to instill debugging and self-repair behavior over Rust code; and (3) task-specific SFT on paired C/Rust solutions derived from LeetCode problems to teach direct semantic translation. We evaluate the resulting model using the agentic, static-analysis-guided verification framework of SACTOR, which performs structure-aware, two-phase (unidiomatic to idiomatic) translation with foreign-function-interface (FFI)-based end-to-end (E2E) testing. We report success rate, idiomaticity (Clippy lint counts, unsafe-code fraction), and failure-mode analyses, and compare our fine-tuned model against baseline Qwen3-27B and other LLMs evaluated under the same framework.

</details>


### [11] [Building AI-Intensive Software with AI: Early Results and a Cautionary Tale on Measuring Development Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13730)
*Victor Barros de Miranda Neves,Kiev Santos da Gama,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项学生团队使用AI辅助开发对话式入职助手的案例研究，开发了一个三层成本模型，初始报告19.4x的成本比，后续修正两个错误（订阅制下推断per-token成本、错误区域劳动定价）后修正为约9.9x，并指出这两种错误在类似报告中可能普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI密集型软件开发的真实成本实证报告十分稀少，且现有少数报告容易在最终数字上犯错误而不被发现，需要更加严谨的成本核算方法论。

Method: 采用案例研究方法：6人学生团队在一学期内使用AI辅助构建完整的对话式入职助手（基于RAG的代码聊天、引导式导览、依赖图、技术债分析）；使用三层成本模型（真实AI支出、自报人力投入、人类反事实成本）进行计量；随后对初始结果进行复查修正。

Result: 初始报告19.4x成本比，经复查发现两个独立错误：在固定费率订阅下推断per-token成本，以及使用错误区域劳动力价格计算反事实成本，两者合计虚增约2倍，修正后约为9.9x。

Conclusion: 该修正本身即是一个早期可推广的发现：这两个错误容易犯、在最终数字中不可见、且在类似报告中可能普遍存在；下一步应开发更稳健、可复现的AI密集型开发成本核算方法。

Abstract: Empirical reports on the true cost of AI-intensive software development remain scarce, and the few that exist are easy to get wrong in ways that never surface in the final number. We report early results from an ongoing case study: a six-person student team built a full conversational onboarding assistant -- RAG-based code chat, guided tours, dependency graphs, technical-debt analysis -- over one academic term using pervasive AI assistance. We instrumented development with a three-layer cost model (real AI spend, self-reported human effort, human counterfactual) and initially reported a 19.4x cost ratio. A follow-up pass revealed two independent errors -- inferring per-token cost under a flat-rate subscription, and pricing the counterfactual with the wrong regional labor rates -- that together had inflated the ratio by roughly 2x; the corrected figure is ~9.9x. We present this correction as an early, generalizable finding in its own right: both errors are easy to make, invisible in the final number, and plausibly common in similar reports. We outline next steps toward a more robust, replicable costing methodology for AI-intensive development.

</details>


### [12] [Does ISO-Grounded NFR Specification Improve LLM Code Generation? A Comparison of Rich and Structured Interventions against a Natural-Language Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13742)
*Joào Pedro Monteiro Pereira,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨将LLM代码生成中的非功能需求（NFR）规范基于ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行丰富（富自然语言散文或结构化JSON）是否能改善代码生成质量。结果显示：ISO基础丰富化改善了静态质量代理指标（如不可读性密度下降）并降低了对提示措辞的敏感性，但不能可靠提升功能正确性；在ISO内容一致时，散文与JSON格式差异可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于LLM的代码生成中，非功能需求（NFR）通常以简短一行短语形式给出，这种简洁规范可能不足以引导模型生成满足质量要求的代码。作者质疑将NFR规范基于ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行丰富化是否能提升生成代码的质量，从而为实践者提供关于NFR提示设计的有据可依的指导。

Method: 在HumanEval/HumanEval-ET基准上，对比三种NFR规范条件：RobuNFR风格的一行基线（NL-simple）、ISO/IEC 25010质量模型基础的富自然语言散文（NL-rich）、以及结构化JSON（Structured）。评估四个NFR维度（性能、错误处理、代码异味、可读性），每种条件使用十个提示变体，在固定模型快照下进行配对非参数统计分析。

Result: 主要发现：ISO基础丰富化改善了静态质量代理指标（四个NFR的不可读性密度均下降，如性能维度从0.88降至0.69），并降低了对提示措辞的敏感性，但未能可靠提升功能正确性；错误处理的扩展测试通过率反而下降，显示防御性编码模式与精确输出基准之间存在张力。次要发现：ISO内容一致时，NL-rich与Structured在正确性上差异极小（|delta|<=0.023），表明语义内容比格式形式更重要。

Conclusion: 实践者应将投入集中在标准基础化的NFR语义内容上，而非关注序列化格式（JSON vs 散文）；需要权衡防御性编码模式与功能正确性之间的张力。研究提供了完全可追溯的复制包。

Abstract: In LLM-based code generation, Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) are often specified as terse one-line phrases. We ask whether grounding those specifications in ISO/IEC 25010 Quality Model, either as rich natural-language prose (NL-rich) or as structured JSON (Structured), improves code generated on HumanEval/HumanEval-ET compared to a RobuNFR-style one-line baseline (NL-simple). We evaluate four NFRs (performance, error handling, code smell, readability) with ten prompt variations per condition under a fixed model snapshot and paired non-parametric analysis. Primary finding: ISO-grounded enrichment improves static quality proxies (unreadability density falls across all four NFRs (e.g., Performance 0.88 -> 0.69 for NL-rich)) and reduces sensitivity to prompt wording, but does not reliably improve functional correctness; for error handling, extended-test pass rate decreases, suggesting tension between defensive coding patterns and exact-output benchmarks. Secondary finding: when ISO content is held constant, NL-rich and Structured differ negligibly in correctness (|delta| <= 0.023), indicating that semantic content matters more than JSON-vs-prose format. Practitioners should invest in standard-grounded NFR content rather than serialization form. A fully traceable replication package is provided.

</details>


### [13] [Engineering Reliable Coding Agents: Evaluating and Operating the System Around the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13867)
*Stephanie Jarmak*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文档对AI编码智能体的可靠性进行了系统级审视，提出将评估与运维视为依赖链的框架，通过多声部综述整合164篇学术文献、100份实践记录、29份基准记录和17份作者系统案例，贡献了包含206条可靠性记录的版本化目录及相关方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体通常以模型形式被评估，但以系统形式被部署，可靠性不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于harness、执行状态、检索、记忆与状态管理、权限、审查界面和资源分配等系统组件。许多表面上的模型故障实际源于系统其他层面，单一层面的改进往往无法传导至端到端结果，因此需要一套系统级方法论来区分模型能力与基础设施效应。

Method: 采用结构化多声部综述（multivocal review）方法，综合学术文献、实践记录、基准记录和作者系统案例四类证据来源；辅以定向更新审计、软件工程覆盖分析和分布式系统证据综合。将评估与运维建模为依赖链，分析任务构建、执行环境、检索、状态管理、验证和可观测性各环节对下游结论的失效影响。

Result: 产出成果包括：206条可靠性记录的版本化目录（193条门控实践，其中56条深入开发，另13条研究线索）；证据账本；覆盖智能体生命周期依赖与修复不对称性的框架；来自实际运行系统的测量与失败案例；可运行的评估与可靠性协议；五个带证据映射的可复用智能体技能。

Conclusion: 该综述是结构化而非穷尽式的，证据强度因主题而异，结果依赖工作负载和配置。需要区分模型能力与基础设施效应、设计可辩护的评估，并构建在组件失败时能安全恢复的系统。方法部分记录了已执行和未执行的搜索通道及其对证据分级结论的限制。

Abstract: AI coding agents are commonly evaluated as models but deployed as systems. Their reliability depends not only on model capability, but on the harness, execution state, retrieval, memory and state management, permissions, review interfaces, and resource allocation. This monograph examines those boundaries and develops a framework for evaluating and operating coding agents reliably. It synthesizes 164 scholarly works, 100 practitioner records, 29 benchmark records, and 17 author-system case records through a structured multivocal review, targeted update audits, software-engineering coverage analysis, and distributed-systems evidence synthesis. Across this evidence, many apparent model failures originate elsewhere in the system, while improvements at one layer often fail to propagate to end-to-end outcomes. Evaluation and operation are treated as a dependency chain in which weaknesses in task construction, execution environments, retrieval, state management, verification, or observability can invalidate downstream conclusions. The monograph contributes a versioned catalog of 206 reliability records: 193 gated practices, including 56 developed in depth, plus 13 research leads; an evidence ledger; a framework for dependency and repair asymmetry across the agent lifecycle; measurements and failure cases from operated agent systems; runnable evaluation and reliability protocols; and five reusable agent skills with evidence maps. Together, these provide a system-level methodology for distinguishing model capability from infrastructure effects, designing defensible evaluations, and building systems that recover safely when components fail. The review is structured rather than exhaustive, evidence strength varies by topic, and results depend on workload and configuration. The methods record which search lanes were executed, which remain unexecuted, and limits on evidence-grading claims.

</details>


### [14] [Engineering Signals of Human-AI Collaboration in the Agentic Coding Era: A Longitudinal Analysis of 33,228 Pull Requests from vLLM and SGLang with Implications for Biomedical AI Agents and Bioinformatics Pipeline Developmen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13884)
*Jiada Li,Xuesong Ye,Olamide Olowoniyi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对两个高活跃度AI基础设施开源仓库（vLLM和SGLang）进行了纵向描述性分析，追踪七个工程指标在AI辅助开发时代的变化。研究发现PR吞吐量增长约18-21倍且主要由人类驱动（机器人提交PR占比不足0.2%），评论密度增长约4倍，月度贡献者持续增加，而PR大小保持稳定。研究将开发过程划分为四个与AI辅助开发变化相对应的时代，表明AI辅助开发与更高的开发速度、更广泛的参与度以及更强的AI-开发者协作信号相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手和自主智能体开发系统的快速普及与开源软件工程速度和结构的重大变化同步发生，但团队层面的纵向实证证据仍然稀缺。作者旨在通过纵向量化分析填补这一空白，揭示AI辅助开发对开源团队实际工程指标的影响。

Method: 对vLLM（2023年2月-2026年6月，18,290个PR）和SGLang（2024年1月-2026年6月，14,938个PR）两个仓库的所有合并PR计算七项工程指标：PR吞吐量、周期时间、贡献者多样性、PR评论密度、合并率、新作者参与度和PR大小。将开发过程按AI辅助开发的重大变化划分为四个时代，并区分人类与机器人作者的活动。

Result: 两个项目均显示开发速度和AI-开发者协作信号显著增加：vLLM的PR吞吐量增长21倍，SGLang增长17.9倍，其中机器人提交的PR不足增长的0.2%，说明增长主要来自人类。最新时代的周期时间中位数分别为1.04天（vLLM）和0.62天（SGLang），P90周期时间为16.8天和14.3天。月度贡献者稳步增加。PR评论密度分别增长4.2倍和3.8倍，机器人评论贡献约15-20%的增长。PR大小在各时代保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发与高活跃度开源软件开发中更高的吞吐量、更广泛的贡献者参与度以及增加的AI-开发者协作信号相关联，但这种增长主要是人类驱动的，AI工具的作用更多体现在增强而非替代人类开发者的协作与产出效率。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding assistants and autonomous agentic development systems has coincided with major changes in the pace and structure of open-source software engineering. Yet empirical longitudinal evidence of these changes at the team level remains limited. We present a descriptive longitudinal analysis of seven engineering metrics: pull request (PR) throughput, cycle time, contributor diversity, PR comment density, merge rate, new-author participation, and PR size. Metrics were computed from all merged PRs in two high-velocity AI infrastructure repositories, vLLM (February 2023-June 2026; 18,290 PRs) and SGLang (January 2024-June 2026; 14,938 PRs). We segment development into four eras aligned with major changes in AI-assisted software development and examine human- and bot-authored activities. Both projects show substantial increases in development velocity and AI-developer collaboration signals. PR throughput increased 21x in vLLM and 17.9x in SGLang, while bot-authored PRs accounted for less than 0.2% of this growth, indicating that the increase was overwhelmingly human-driven. In the latest era, median cycle time was 1.04 days for vLLM and 0.62 days for SGLang, while P90 cycle times reached 16.8 and 14.3 days, respectively. Monthly unique authors increased steadily in both projects, suggesting broader contributor participation. PR comment density increased 4.2x in vLLM and 3.8x in SGLang, with bot comments contributing an estimated 15-20% of the increase. In contrast, PR size remained relatively stable across eras. Overall, AI-assisted development is associated with higher throughput, broader contributor participation, and increased AI-developer collaboration signals in high-velocity open-source software development.

</details>


### [15] [Rethinking Automated Program Repair: The Impact of Bug Complexity, Fault Localization, and LLM Cost-efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14065)
*Junchi Liu,Ali Bigdeli,Roya Daneshi,Atu Ambala,Sudipto Ghosh,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动程序修复（APR）技术进行了全面的实证分析，评估了ChatRepair和CodeCorrector两种APR技术在不同LLM（DeepSeek、GPT、Llama）上的表现，重点关注bug复杂度、故障定位精度、推理设置和成本效益等因素对修复性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based APR研究主要关注整体修复效果，对bug复杂度、故障定位、推理设置以及修复成本效益等因素的影响缺乏深入探讨。本研究旨在填补这一空白，提供多维度的实证分析。

Method: 采用多维度实证框架和统计分析，评估两种APR技术（ChatRepair和CodeCorrector）与三种LLM（DeepSeek、GPT、Llama）的组合，在不同bug复杂度和故障定位策略下的修复性能。

Result: 结构复杂的bug和不精确的故障定位会增加修复难度，但LLM-based APR仍能实现有竞争力的修复效果。不精确的故障定位会显著扩大APR技术间的性能差距。更昂贵的LLM和更强的推理设置并不总能带来更好的成本效益。GPT-5比DeepSeek-V4-pro多修复7个复杂bug，比DeepSeek-V3.2多修复39个；但DeepSeek-V3.2在总修复成本上成本效益最佳。

Conclusion: 超过50%的中等复杂bug可由低成本LLM-based APR技术修复。APR技术间的修复效果差距随故障定位精度降低而增大。修复效果与计算成本之间存在非平凡的权衡关系，成本更高的LLM不一定更划算。

Abstract: Background: Software bugs remain a critical challenge in development, necessitating effective Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based APR systems have shown promise, prior studies primarily focus on overall repair effectiveness. The effects of bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and repair cost-effectiveness remain insufficiently explored.
  Aims: This study presents a comprehensive empirical analysis of LLM-based APR, focusing on how repair performance is shaped by bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and costs.
  Method: We evaluate two APR techniques (ChatRepair and CodeCorrector) using three LLMs (DeepSeek, GPT, and Llama), and examine their performance across diverse levels of bug complexity and localization strategies through a multi-dimensional empirical framework and statistical analysis.
  Results: Although structurally complex bugs and imprecise fault localization make repair more challenging, LLM-based APR techniques still achieve competitive repair effectiveness. Imprecise fault localization can substantially enlarge the performance gap between APR techniques. Furthermore, higher-cost LLMs and stronger reasoning settings do not consistently yield better cost-efficiency, revealing a nontrivial trade-off between repair effectiveness and computational cost.
  Conclusions: Over 50% of moderately complex bugs can be repaired by low-cost LLM-based APR techniques. The repair effectiveness gap between APR techniques becomes larger as fault localization becomes less precise. GPT-5 repairs 7 and 39 more complex bugs than DeepSeek-V4-pro and DeepSeek-V3.2, respectively; whereas the total repair cost of DeepSeek-V3.2 shows the best cost-efficiency performance.

</details>


### [16] [DepWareTrans: Dependency-Aware Incremental Repository Migration across Co-executable Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14128)
*Sivajeet Chand,Alexander Pretschner,Steve Haupt,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种依赖感知的增量迁移框架，将代码翻译单元从文件级别提升到依赖一致的批次级别，解决仓库级代码翻译中跨文件依赖不一致的问题。在51K行代码的工业系统STAR等测试中，该方法实现了100%的编译和测试成功率，远超文件级方法的38.16%编译和9.39%测试成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的代码翻译方法在文件级别操作，无法扩展到具有复杂跨文件依赖的代码库。在工业场景中（将STAR仓库从Java迁移到Kotlin），文件级方法产生碎片化结果，无法实现端到端的正确性。研究发现仓库级别失败的主要原因是依赖不一致——大多数错误源于无法通过迭代反馈有效解决的未解析跨文件依赖。

Method: 提出依赖感知的增量迁移框架，核心思想是将翻译单元从单个文件提升为依赖一致的批次。具体步骤包括：(1) 构建依赖图；(2) 将相互依赖的文件分组； (3) 执行批处理翻译；(4) 通过迭代编译和测试驱动的验证进行校正。

Result: 在51K行代码的工业系统STAR以及多个互操作语言对（Java-Kotlin、Java-Scala、C#-F#）的多个仓库上进行了评估。在STAR仓库上，文件级方法仅达到38.16%的编译成功率和9.39%的测试成功率，而该方法在所有评估设置中达到100%的编译和测试成功率，并在少量迭代内收敛。

Conclusion: 依赖感知的批处理方式显著提高了仓库级代码翻译的可扩展性和可靠性，证明了将翻译单元从文件级别提升到依赖一致性批次级别的有效性。

Abstract: Repository-level code translation is critical for modernizing legacy systems, yet existing approaches based on large language models (LLMs) operate at the file level and fail to scale to codebases with complex inter-file dependencies. This limitation is evident in our industrial setting, where we aim to migrate a production repository (STAR) from Java to Kotlin, but file-level approaches produce fragmented results and fail to achieve end-to-end correctness. In this paper, we show that the primary cause of failure at the repository level is dependency inconsistency. Through an empirical study on open-source and industrial systems, we find that most errors arise from unresolved cross-file dependencies that cannot be effectively addressed by iterative feedback alone. We propose a dependency-aware incremental migration framework that elevates the unit of translation from individual files to dependency-consistent batches. Our approach constructs a dependency graph, groups interdependent files, and performs batched translation with iterative compile- and test-driven validation. We evaluate our method on a 51K line of code (LOC) industrial system and multiple repositories across interoperable language pairs (Java-Kotlin, Java-Scala, and C#-F#). On the STAR repository, file-level approaches achieve 38.16% compilation and 9.39% test success, whereas our approach achieves 100% compilation and test success across the evaluated settings, converging within a small number of iterations. These results show that dependency-aware batching improves scalability and reliability in repository-level code translation.

</details>


### [17] [LegacyWorld: Atomicity-Aware Evaluation of GUI Agents for Legacy Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14131)
*Thilo Reintjes,Sivajeet Chand,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Alexander Pretschner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了面向遗留系统GUI工作流自动化的多模态LLM智能体框架"legacy-use"的预部署评估研究，提出用"原子性"（atomicity）作为核心评价指标——智能体运行要么正确完成任务，要么在无副作用的情况下安全失败。基于28个Windows GUI工作流基准，对比专家提示词与屏幕录制生成提示词，发现有用完成、安全失败和非原子副作用是三种不同的操作画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留企业系统因关键工作流缺乏可编程接口、仍依赖手动GUI交互而难以现代化。现有演示成功不等于实际可靠：智能体运行失败后可能在企业或医疗记录中留下持久且无效的变更。因此需要一种能识别这类有状态工作流并在部署前评估原子性（要么完整完成且正确，要么无意外持久副作用地失败）的方法。

Method: 构建了一个由领域专家指导的基准，包含28个Windows GUI工作流，每个工作流指定了初始状态、目标状态和任务特定验证器。对比了专家手工编写的提示词与从专家黄金路径屏幕录制生成的提示词，并在六个托管计算机使用（computer-use）智能体上进行系统化评估。

Result: 跨越六个托管计算机使用智能体的实验结果揭示了三种不同的操作画像：有用完成（useful completion）、安全失败（safe failure）和非原子副作用（non-atomic side effects）。这些维度是可区分的性能指标，而非单一的成功率可以涵盖的。

Conclusion: 工作流捕获（workflow capture）、状态验证器（state validators）和原子性感知的验收测试（atomicity-aware acceptance tests）应作为基于AI的遗留工作流自动化的第一类需求，而非附加的工程实践。

Abstract: Legacy and legacy-like enterprise systems often remain difficult to modernize because critical workflows expose limited programmable interfaces and still require manual GUI interaction. This paper reports a pre-deployment evaluation study motivated by the development of legacy-use, an industry-oriented framework for automating such workflows with multimodal LLM agents. During framework development, domain experts helped identify stateful workflows where successful demos are not sufficient: a failed agent run may still leave persistent invalid changes in business or healthcare records. We therefore evaluate computer-use agents using atomicity: a run should either complete the intended workflow correctly or fail without unintended persistent side effects. We construct a domain-expert-informed benchmark of 28 Windows GUI workflows, each specified with an initial state, goal state, and task-specific validator. We compare expert-crafted prompts with prompts generated from screen recordings of expert golden-path executions. Across six hosted computer-use agents, our results show that useful completion, safe failure, and non-atomic side effects are distinct operational profiles. We conclude that workflow capture, state validators, and atomicity-aware acceptance tests should be first-class requirements for AI-based legacy workflow automation.

</details>


### [18] [Breaking Models to Test the Judge: A Mutation Testing Approach for Semantic Evaluators of Domain Class Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14315)
*Kevin Delcourt,Meriem Ben Chaaben,Abdelhamid Rouatbi,Luciano Marchezan,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于突变测试的方法，通过向领域类图中注入受控的语义缺陷，来规模化评估语义评判器（如基于LLM的评判器）的性能，而无需依赖昂贵且主观的人工判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软件工程中，许多语义建模任务缺乏唯一ground truth，人工评估既昂贵又主观。缺乏可扩展的替代方案来评估语义评判器的有效性。

Method: 从（PlantUML类图+文本系统描述）的配对出发，设计11个突变算子（如删除类）向类图中注入受控语义缺陷，生成故障变体；候选评判器通过检测这些注入缺陷的能力进行评估，并与传统人工评估结果进行对照。

Result: 在6种评判器配置（3个LLM×2种提示词变体）下，自动突变测试方法与人工评估在识别更优配置方面大体一致。

Conclusion: 突变测试可以作为分析语义评判器的一种可扩展的代理方法。

Abstract: In software engineering, many semantic modeling tasks lack a unique ground truth, as human judgments are both costly and subjective. This paper explores mutation testing as a scalable alternative for evaluating semantic judges (e.g., LLM-based) of models. We propose a mutation testing approach in which controlled semantic defects are injected into domain class diagrams. Starting from pairs of PlantUML class diagrams and textual system descriptions, we apply mutation operators (e.g., removing a class) to generate faulty variants. A candidate judge is then evaluated based on its ability to detect the injected defects. We define 11 mutation operators for the task of comparing a domain class diagram against a textual description and evaluate the proposed approach against a conventional manual assessment of judgment validity. Across six judge configurations (three LLMs and two prompt variants), the automated mutation testing approach is largely consistent with the manual assessment in identifying the better-performing configurations. The results suggest that mutation testing may serve as a scalable proxy for analyzing semantic judges.

</details>


### [19] [ATLAS: Discovering Agent Strategies through LLM-Guided Abstraction and Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito,Ezio Bartocci,Bettina Könighofer,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATLAS是一种从LLM智能体轨迹中自动学习可解释有限状态模型的方法，结合轨迹抽象与自动机学习，能从渗透测试智能体的行为中提取高层策略，支持可解释性、模型引导探索、审计分析以及模型间知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在复杂任务中表现强大，但其行为难以理解、解释和分析。现有评估主要关注任务成功率和执行轨迹，无法提供对智能体所采用策略的深入洞察。

Method: ATLAS将轨迹抽象（trace abstraction）与自动机学习（automata learning）相结合，从智能体与环境交互的轨迹中推断出有限状态模型，以捕获可观测的交互策略。该模型提供人类可解释的洞察，并支持对重复行为、决策点、成功路径和失败循环的自动化分析。

Result: 将ATLAS应用于LLM渗透测试智能体的轨迹后，获得了难以从原始执行痕迹中识别的高层行为策略。在包含12台易受攻击机器的案例研究中展示了模型变换派生简洁行为解释的能力，并演示了从强大前沿模型到紧凑语言模型的基于符号模型的知识迁移。

Conclusion: ATLAS为模型驱动工程开辟了新机会：将智能体轨迹转化为显式行为模型，从而实现对原本不透明的AI智能体进行系统性理解与分析，支持可解释性、审计、探索和知识迁移等多种应用场景。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for complex tasks such as software testing and cybersecurity assessment. While these agents demonstrate impressive capabilities, their behavior is difficult to understand, explain, and analyze. Existing evaluations focus mainly on task success and execution traces, offering limited insight into the strategies employed by the agent. We present ATLAS (Automata Learning for Agent Trajectory Analysis and Strategy Discovery), an approach for recovering interpretable behavioral models from agent trajectories. ATLAS combines trace abstraction with automata learning to infer finite-state models that capture observed agent-environment interaction strategies. These models provide human-interpretable insights and support automated analyses of recurring behaviors, decision points, successful task-completion paths, and failure loops. As a proof of concept, we apply ATLAS to trajectories generated by an LLM-based penetration-testing agent. The resulting models expose high-level behavioral strategies for exploiting vulnerable machines that are difficult to identify from raw execution traces alone. We discuss how learned behavioral models can support explainability, model-guided exploration, auditing, and analysis of agentic systems. We further demonstrate symbolic model-based knowledge transfer from powerful frontier models to compact language models. In addition, we show how model transformations can derive concise explanations of agent behavior in a penetration-testing case study comprising 12 vulnerable machines. ATLAS highlights a new opportunity for model-driven engineering: transforming agent trajectories into explicit behavioral models that enable systematic understanding and analysis of otherwise opaque AI agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [20] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lorentzian Fourier神经算子（L-FNO），一种用于稀疏事件动力学建模的随机神经算子，结合FNO风格的协变量路径、Lorentzian谱核进行历史依赖激励，并采用基于似然的训练目标，在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集上优于回归与似然类神经算子基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代运筹系统即使在常规条件下也存在不确定性，罕见事件具有突发性、自激励性特征，这些事件由外生协变量和内生事件动态共同驱动。标准的神经算子通常被训练为回归式的函数到函数映射模型，而非条件强度估计器，这限制了它们在稀疏事件场景中的适用性。

Method: L-FNO由三个关键组件构成：（1）FNO风格的协变量路径，用于处理外生特征；（2）Lorentzian谱核，用于建模历史依赖的激励效应；（3）基于似然的训练目标，替代传统的回归式损失函数，从而能够估计条件强度而非仅做函数逼近。

Result: 在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集（涵盖疾病暴发预测和半导体故障/缺陷检测）上，L-FNO在事件似然度、校准诊断指标和罕见事件检测方面均优于回归式和似然式神经算子基线。

Conclusion: 结构化谱记忆与基于似然的学习为神经算子模型提供了有效的归纳偏置，能够更准确地建模随机事件动力学。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics. Standard neural operators are typically trained as regression-style function-to-function models rather than conditional-intensity estimators, limiting their suitability for sparse event regimes. We introduce the Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO), a stochastic neural operator that combines an FNO-style covariate path, Lorentzian spectral kernels for history-dependent excitation, and a likelihood-based training objective. We evaluate L-FNO on eight synthetic point-process benchmarks and three real-world datasets covering disease outbreak prediction and semiconductor fault or defect detection. L-FNO improves event likelihood, calibration diagnostics, and rare-event detection over regression- and likelihood-based neural operator baselines. These results show that structured spectral memory and likelihood-based learning provide effective inductive biases for neural operator models of stochastic event dynamics.

</details>


### [21] [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566)
*Egor Shibaev,Vera Kudrevskaia,Timur Galimzyanov,Mikhail Evtikhiev,Ana Terna,Rastislav Rabatin,Timur Kudashev,Timofey Bryksin,Arina Puchkova,Patrik Bartak,Egor Bogomolov,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出仅用少量编码基准（如SWE-bench、LiveCodeBench）评估模型编码能力存在"意义差距"——基准分数不能代表通用编码能力。作者用Django案例研究基准套件验证了这一现象，发现后训练checkpoint在基准间缺乏跨任务迁移，SWE-bench优化在Django任务和LiveCodeBench上几乎无增益，最终建议采用差异化评估策略、构建能力分类体系并持续维护基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有论文、模型卡片和博客常将少量编码基准的分数视为广泛编码能力的证据，但针对基准进行优化会测量任务特定性能，造成分数与通用编码能力主张之间的意义差距，亟需系统性地检验和解决这一问题。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于Django框架的案例研究基准测试套件，评估了在SWE-bench轨迹上进行后训练的基金会模型和checkpoint，比较其在Django任务、LiveCodeBench以及SWE-bench上的表现，同时检验了单个Django模态微调的迁移效果。

Result: 基准排名经常无法泛化；后训练checkpoint显示出极少的跨任务迁移；SWE-bench优化在Django任务和LiveCodeBench上只有有限或无增益；针对单个Django模态的微调同样无法迁移。

Conclusion: 少量基准不足以在基准优化压力下评估多样化模型。建议采用差异化评估（前沿模型用整体评估、研究用多任务套件、窄任务用人在回环研究），并呼吁建立能力分类体系和持续基准维护，而非一次性发布基准。

Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [22] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究XGBoost的稳健性，发现其性能受垂直离群点和杠杆点影响，提出基于稳健回归中M-、S-和τ估计量的替代损失函数，其中两阶段的MM-XGBoost方法在稳健性和预测精度之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost作为广泛使用的预测方法，其标准损失函数（二次损失）对异常值敏感，垂直离群点和杠杆点会显著影响其性能，需要更稳健的损失函数设计。

Method: 探索基于稳健回归中M估计器、S估计器和τ估计器的替代损失函数，并特别提出一种两步MM-XGBoost方法，以在保持预测精度的同时提升对异常值的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果显示，MM-XGBoost这一两步方法在稳健性与预测精度之间提供了最佳的权衡表现。

Conclusion: MM-XGBoost是应对异常值场景下XGBoost性能退化的有效方案，为提升梯度提升树的稳健性提供了新思路。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [23] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Activation-Prune-Merge（APM）框架，通过激活引导的方式将大型模型裁剪为小型接收模型的架构，并以微小的混合系数注入，实现跨尺度异构模型融合。在16个基准测试中将3B接收模型的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%，证明无需显式的神经元级语义对齐即可实现跨规模能力迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构模型融合通常面对任务、初始化、架构或规模上的差异，而跨尺度（大模型增强小模型）这一场景尚未被充分探索。作者想要回答一个核心问题：在存在显著架构不匹配的情况下，是否可以不依赖显式的神经元级语义对齐，就能从强模型向弱模型转移有用能力。此前已有观察表明，将大模型截断为较小架构并以微小混合权重注入即可改善接收模型，这为探索更系统的激活引导融合方法提供了动机。

Method: APM框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 在供体模型上构建任务条件化的激活图谱（activation maps）；(2) 基于这些激活图谱选择重要的层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元，将供体裁剪为与接收模型匹配的架构（Activation-Prune）；(3) 利用微插值系数将裁剪后的供体切片注入原始接收模型（Merge）。该方法将供体视为"集中功能组件"的来源，而非要求精确的结构移植。

Result: 在涵盖推理、数学、代码生成、指令跟随和分类的16个基准测试中，APM将原始3B接收模型的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。具体而言，RTE准确率从64.3%提升至82.3%，QNLI从52.3%提升至65.7%，BoolQ从70.8%提升至79.2%。注入比例分析和顺序多阶段融合实验进一步表明，激活引导提取提高了可迁移供体切片的质量，同时保持了小比例融合的机制。

Conclusion: 当供体的贡献足够集中且经过精心选择时，跨尺度异构融合可以在不进行显式语义对齐的情况下成功实现。这一发现为异构模型融合提供了新的思路，即通过激活引导的稀疏化提取来替代精确的结构对齐，从而更有效地实现大模型到小模型的能力迁移。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [24] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对照实验检验不确定性采样是否因难以标签的样本而产生劣势，发现其对干净标签数据有明确增益，但在有噪声时其稳健性高度依赖数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习通过选择信息量最大的样本来降低标注成本，但最不确定的样本往往也是最难正确标注的。研究旨在区分不确定性采样失败的两个可能原因：获取了更多损坏标签，还是错误的聚集在困难区域尤其有害。

Method: 在三个公开二分类表格数据集上，将基于边距的不确定性采样与随机采样进行比较，涵盖干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）和有界难度依赖噪声三种设置。采用100对配对种子，9个期望噪声率（0-0.30），标注预算20-120，并在每个预算下通过交叉验证重新选择正则化参数的逻辑回归。设计了暴露匹配的RCN对照以对齐平均最终获取的损坏程度，并扩展到预算400的干净标签实验。

Result: 在干净标签下，不确定性采样在所有数据集上将归一化平衡准确率的曲线下面积提高了1.09至1.77个百分点。难度依赖噪声在乳腺癌威斯康星数据集8个噪声率中的6个上比RCN更大地削弱了这一优势，但在其他两个数据集上任何噪声率下均无此效应。暴露匹配分析未发现结构化错误位置带来的普遍额外惩罚。在干净的MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样提高了平衡准确率，但降低了平均精确率和固定假阳性率下的真正例率。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样在标注效率上是有效的，但其表面上的稳健性取决于数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标，不能得出其存在普遍性额外惩罚的结论。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [25] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KRPRVFL模型，将核风险敏感均值p幂（KRP）准则引入随机向量功能链接（RVFL）网络，替代标准最小二乘目标函数，以增强模型对噪声标签、离群点和不平衡数据的鲁棒性，并通过协作学习机制和核诱导特征映射进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL网络虽然轻量高效、具备良好的泛化能力，但对噪声标签、离群点和数据不平衡非常敏感，在真实应用场景中性能受限。现有方法缺乏针对这些挑战的有效解决方案，因此需要一种既能保持RVFL计算效率又能提升鲁棒性的模型。

Method: 提出KRPRVFL模型，核心方法包括：(1) 用核风险敏感均值p幂（KRP）准则替换标准最小二乘目标函数，自适应降低损坏或不可靠样本的训练影响；(2) 引入协作学习机制，实现模型组件间的自适应交互以增强鲁棒性；(3) 利用核诱导特征映射捕捉非线性关系，无需显式选择隐藏层，保持效率与可扩展性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上进行的大量实验表明，KRPRVFL在准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面持续优于基线模型，验证了其作为快速、可扩展且可靠的分类解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL通过融合KRP稳健准则与RVFL的高效架构，有效解决了传统RVFL对噪声和异常数据敏感的问题，为复杂噪声环境下的分类任务提供了一种兼具效率与鲁棒性的可靠方案。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [26] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一个两阶段的事件级异常检测框架：先通过监督对比学习在多样物理过程上学习嵌入空间，再在该空间中运行标准自编码器生成异常分数。在HL-LHC条件下，相比基线自编码器，ORCA在灵敏度的广度和深度上均有显著提升，且对比嵌入使异常样本可解释——通过最大似然模板拟合可归属事件到已知物理过程并量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机物理中通用事件级异常检测存在两个反复出现的问题：异常分数难以解释，且分数与能量标度和粒子多重性强烈相关。这限制了异常检测方法在高能物理搜索中的实际应用价值。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段通过监督对比学习在多样物理过程集合上学习嵌入空间；第二阶段在该嵌入空间中运行标准自编码器以生成事件级异常分数。此外，利用对比嵌入的可解释性，采用最大似然模板拟合并辅以量化不确定性来归属异常事件。

Result: 在模拟的HL-LHC条件下，ORCA相比基线自编码器在检测新物理信号的灵敏度广度和深度上均有显著提升。模板拟合能准确恢复注入信号产额（包括训练中未见的信号），并能通过最相似的已知过程来刻画模板库中不存在的信号。

Conclusion: ORCA为对撞机物理中可解释的异常检测搜索提供了一条可行路径：嵌入几何承载了比标准一维输出拟合更高维的物理信息，增强了后续统计分析能力。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [27] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出查询衍生擦除方向（QED），在线性注意力模型中增加一个由查询而非键推导的、与键正交的第二擦除方向，以解决长上下文下键向擦除无法消除读取干扰的问题，从而显著延长可用上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力在长上下文下因固定大小状态中存存储过多条目而产生干扰，导致检索退化。现有的delta-rule模型（包括Gated DeltaNet-2）擦除向量均由当前token的键推导，但读取干扰是通过查询衡量的，键向擦除无法触及查询方向上的干扰，这是一个根本性的设计缺陷。

Method: QED引入由查询推导且与键正交的第二擦除方向。在快速权重视角下，键向的delta编辑无法改变读取中与键正交的部分，因此用键可编辑部分来抵消沿查询度量的旧状态内容，有效消除查询方向的干扰。

Result: QED在训练窗口之外的每个长度上都改善了检索性能，并在S-NIAH-1基准上将可用上下文长度大约提升了两倍（翻倍）。

Conclusion: 识别了delta-rule线性注意力中键向擦除与查询向读取之间的不对齐问题，并通过查询衍生的正交擦除方向弥补这一差距，显著提升了长上下文检索能力。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [28] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文档概述了2018年10月至2026年7月间AI模型从简单系统（如BERT）演化为可解决复杂数学和编写软件的智能体的历程。真实编码问题的解决能力自2024年底以来每年提升近6倍，同时成本大幅下降（如GPT-5.6-Luna以1-6美元/百万token的价格匹敌旗舰模型）。顶级性能已分散到多个专业化模型（Claude Opus 5擅长前端编码、Claude Fable 5擅长仓库级编码、GPT-5.6-Sol擅长终端任务）。在基准测试中，高级采样方法将解决率从58%提升至79%，置信度排序工具在Top50内正确识别47个正确答案，所有研究材料均已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪并量化AI模型在性能和成本两方面的快速演进趋势，以及模型专业化分工带来的能力分布变化，同时验证高级推理采样方法和置信度排序工具在实际基准测试中的有效性。

Method: 通过时间序列对比（2018-2026）分析AI编码能力的提升幅度与推理成本下降趋势；在Qwen-2.5模型的初中数学测试中，对比基础方法与高级采样的解题率差异；使用置信度排序工具对答案进行排名评估其筛选准确率。

Result: 自2024年底以来，真实编码问题解决能力每年提升约6倍；GPT-5.6-Luna以极低成本（1-6美元/百万token）匹配旗舰模型性能。不同模型在各自专业领域（前端编码、仓库级编码、终端任务）表现最佳。高级采样将数学解题率从58%提升至79%，置信度排序工具在Top50中正确识别47个正确答案。

Conclusion: AI模型正进入高性能与低成本并行、专业分化加速的发展阶段。高级采样方法和置信度排序工具能显著提升窄域任务（如数学推理）的准确率，且所有研究数据已公开以促进可复现性。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [29] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG-PRISM是一种通用的事后可解释性方法，通过线性变换将EEG基础模型的时间-通道归因分数映射到频率域和源空间域，无需修改或重训练底层模型，实现了对EEG基础模型在生理学相关领域的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型是EEG分析中AI的前沿发展方向，但现有可解释AI技术仅在时间-通道输入空间提供归因分数，与临床对EEG的直觉理解（如频率、脑源）不匹配。因此需要一个通用方法，能将任意基础模型的可解释性扩展到替代的、生理学相关的领域。

Method: EEG-PRISM利用线性变换和既有的反向传播规则，将时间-通道归因分数映射到替代域。具体包括：通过可逆DFT映射到频率域，通过近似可逆的EEG生成模型映射到源空间域。在模拟数据和真实数据上，使用五个基础模型和四个AI解释器进行评估。

Result: 模拟实验中，EEG-PRISM实现近完美的频谱恢复和69.2%的空间准确率。在癫痫数据中，正确判断delta-theta活动最为显著，并以50%准确率定位癫痫发作起始区域。在自闭症数据中，将预测性delta-alpha生物标志物定位到额叶和颞叶区域，与先前研究一致。

Conclusion: EEG-PRISM是一种理论扎实的事后归因方法，能准确映射到频谱和空间域。它支持瞬时事件（如癫痫发作）的窗口级分析和临床相关生物标志物（如自闭症）的组级识别，推进了可解释EEG基础模型的发展，其意义在于实现生理学基础的EEG基础模型解释，支持事件定位和生物标志物识别等临床洞察。

Abstract: Objective: Foundation models represent the next advancement in AI for EEG analysis; however current explainable AI techniques provide attribution scores in the time-channel input space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition about EEG. Thus, there is a critical need for a universal method that can extend the interpretability of any foundation model to alternative and physiologically relevant domains without modifying or retraining the underlying model. Methods: EEG-PRISM leverages linear transformations and established backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into alternative domains. We derive mappings to the frequency domain via an invertible DFT and to the source domain via an approximately invertible EEG generative model. We evaluate EEG-PRISM in simulated and real data, assessing recovery of ground-truth phenomena across domains with five foundation models and four AI explainers. Results: In simulation, EEG-PRISM achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, EEG-PRISM correctly determines that delta-theta activity is most salient and correctly localizes the seizure onset region with 50% accuracy. In autism, EEG-PRISM localizes the predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions, consistent with prior work. Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method with accurate mapping into the spectral and spatial domains. It supports window-level analysis of transient events (e.g., seizures) and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers (e.g., autism), thus advancing interpretable EEG foundation models. Significance: This work enables physiologically-grounded interpretation of EEG foundation models and supports clinically relevant insights such as event localization and biomarker identification.

</details>


### [30] [SAGE: Surrogate-gradient Adaptation via Attention-Guided Entropy for Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13702)
*Kiran Nair,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE，一种基于不确定性的自适应替代梯度机制，用于Transformer类脉冲神经网络（SNN）的训练。SAGE利用归一化自注意力熵估计块级不确定性，并据此在训练期间动态调整替代梯度的斜率，推理时模型不变，在CIFAR-10/100上相比固定替代梯度基线获得1-2%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN凭借稀疏事件驱动计算具备能效优势，但其训练面临挑战：脉冲函数的不可微性需要借助替代梯度，而固定形状的替代梯度在不同层和不同训练阶段可能并非最优，限制了优化灵活性和最终精度。

Method: SAGE从归一化的自注意力熵中估计块级不确定性，并以此信号在训练期间自适应调整替代梯度的斜率，仅改变训练期的替代参数，推理架构和部署成本保持不变。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100数据集上，SAGE相比固定替代梯度基线在多个仿真时间步下实现了1-2%的持续精度提升，验证了基于注意力的不确定性作为轻量训练信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 注意力导出的不确定性可作为Transformer类SNN中自适应替代梯度学习的有效且轻量的训练信号，在提升训练优化灵活性的同时不增加推理开销，为SNN训练方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient alternative to conventional deep neural networks by exploiting sparse event-driven computation, but their training remains challenging because the non-differentiable spike function requires surrogate gradients whose fixed shape may be suboptimal across layers and training stages. In this work, we introduce SAGE, an uncertainty-modulated surrogate-gradient mechanism for Transformer-based SNNs. SAGE estimates block-level uncertainty from normalized self-attention entropy and uses this signal to adapt the surrogate-gradient slope during training while leaving the inference model unchanged. By modulating only the training-time surrogate parameter, the proposed method preserves the original architecture and deployment cost while improving optimization flexibility. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100 demonstrate that SAGE achieves improved accuracy over fixed-surrogate baselines, with results up to 1-2\% consistent gains across multiple simulation time steps. These results highlight the potential of attention-derived uncertainty as a lightweight training signal for adaptive surrogate-gradient learning in transformer-based SNNs.

</details>


### [31] [Capacity-Dependent Effects of Data Selection for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13721)
*Cuong Dang,Hoang Anh Just,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了推理任务监督微调中基于似然度的数据选择假设，发现"高似然数据更好"并不普遍成立，其效果取决于模型容量和训练时长，存在"快速拟合/缓慢增益"的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于似然度的响应选择方法认为更接近学生分布的响应能提供更有效的监督信号，暗示高似然响应通常更优。但这一直觉是否普遍成立缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 通过受控实验，使用1.5B到8B参数的学生模型和更强教师模型生成的数学推理监督数据，分析不同似然度数据在不同模型容量和训练时长下的训练效果，并结合学习动力学分析和容量约束的蒸馏理论视角进行解释。

Result: 发现容量依赖的"Fast-Fit/Slow-Gain"模式：高似然数据对较小模型带来更快更稳定的早期提升，但低似然数据对较大模型在更长训练时长下更有利。小模型往往无法吸收低似然监督而陷入浅层或重复行为，大模型则能更好地向教师分布靠近。

Conclusion: 推理任务的有效数据选择应同时考虑模型容量和计算预算，不存在单一的通用偏好，不能一概认为高似然监督始终更优。

Abstract: In reasoning supervised fine-tuning, candidate responses for the same instruction can differ substantially in how well they match the student's current distribution. Recent likelihood-based response selection methods suggest that responses closer to the student distribution provide more effective supervision, motivating the hypothesis that high-likelihood responses may generally be preferable for fine-tuning. In this paper, we revisit this intuition and show that the value of likelihood-based data selection depends critically on model capacity and training duration. Through controlled experiments on mathematical reasoning, using students ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters and supervision generated by stronger teacher models, we observe a clear \emph{capacity-dependent} ``{\color{SMALLCOLOR}\textbf{Fast-Fit}} / {\color{LARGECOLOR}\textbf{Slow-Gain}}'' pattern. High-likelihood data provides faster and more stable early improvements, especially for smaller models, but low-likelihood data becomes increasingly beneficial for larger models when training is allowed to continue longer. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze learning dynamics, showing that small models often fail to absorb low-likelihood supervision and instead fall into shallow or repetitive behaviors, while larger models are better able to move toward the teacher distribution under such data. We further provide a capacity-constrained theoretical view of distillation that clarifies how data difficulty, data span, and student capacity jointly govern transfer. Overall, our findings show that effective data selection for reasoning should be aware of model capacity and computing budget rather than based on a single universal preference for high-likelihood supervision.

</details>


### [32] [The Integer Alibi: Localizing Cross-Kernel Divergence in INT8-Quantized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13756)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了"相同INT8 GEMM接口的两个GPU内核可互换"这一假设。作者在固定所有其他变量（checkpoint、提示、硬件、推理引擎、解码、量化配置）的情况下，仅交换vLLM中的INT8线性内核（CUTLASS与Triton），发现两个版本在端到端输出上完全不一致（0/8、0/16、0/64序列一致）。进一步分析表明，INT32累加器因为是精确且顺序无关的，不可能是差异来源；差异被定位到缩放应用和输出舍入阶段。通过探针checkpoint干预，可以恢复端到端的逐位一致性（8/8和16/16）。论文附带预注册、逐层预测、清单和一致性验证程序等资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设实现相同接口的两个GPU内核可以互换使用，但这一假设从未被系统验证。作者希望通过严格的受控实验测试该假设，并精确定位内核差异的来源，进而提供可操作的一致性检验方法。

Method: 在固定所有其他变量（checkpoint、提示、硬件、推理引擎、解码、量化配置）的情况下，仅替换vLLM中的INT8线性内核（CUTLASS vs Triton），进行端到端对比；利用INT8点积的精确性和顺序无关性将差异范围缩窄到累加器之后；逐层喂入相同操作数（Qwen3-1.7B的196层和8B的252层）验证输出一致性；设计探针checkpoint干预来定位并验证差异来源；通过teacher-forced replay分析翻转与logit margin的关系；与FP8 GEMM交叉实现对比差异特性。

Result: 两个内核在端到端上没有任何序列一致（0/8、0/16、0/64），但逐层验证在power-of-two缩放下输出完全逐位一致（196/196和252/252），在真实缩放下最大差异为一个bfloat16间距。差异被定位到缩放应用和输出舍入。探针干预恢复端到端一致性（8/8、16/16）。FP8差异随归约深度增长，而INT8差异始终在ppm级别且保持在一个间距内。翻转预测ROC-AUC达0.94。

Conclusion: 相同接口的INT8 GEMM内核在端到端推理中确实不可互换，差异源于精确累加器之后的缩放和舍入阶段。作者提供了完整的预注册、预测、清单和一致性验证程序，使内核可互换性成为一个可检验的属性。

Abstract: Two GPU kernels implementing the same scaled INT8 GEMM interface are usually treated as interchangeable. We test that assumption: holding the checkpoint, prompts, hardware, inference engine, decoding, and quantization configuration fixed, we swap only the INT8 linear kernel (CUTLASS versus Triton) inside vLLM. At 1.7B each arm reproduces itself bit-for-bit across cold restarts, yet the arms agree on no sequence in any end-to-end comparison we ran (0/8, 0/16, and 0/64). What makes this more than a benchmark discrepancy is an integer alibi: for shared INT8 operands under a verified no-overflow bound, the INT32 dot product is exact and order-independent, so the accumulator cannot be the source of any difference. Feeding both kernels identical operands from every linear layer of Qwen3-1.7B and 8B (196 and 252 layers), we find bit-identical outputs under power-of-two scales, confirming a pinned prediction list 196/196 and 252/252 (pre-registered at 1.7B, pinned but not blind at 8B), and observed differences of at most one bfloat16 spacing under the checkpoints' real scales. This localizes the divergence to scale application and output rounding after the exact accumulator. Applied as a probe checkpoint, the same intervention restores end-to-end bitwise agreement (8/8 and 16/16 sequences). Cross-implementation FP8 GEMM shows a different signature: both the prevalence and the magnitude of differences grow with reduction depth, while the INT8 fraction stays at parts per million and within one spacing over a 64x range of K. Teacher-forced replay ties layers to tokens: flips concentrate at small logit margins, which predict flip risk with ROC-AUC 0.94 on 16,384 positions. We will release the pre-registration, per-layer predictions, manifests with kernel-selection evidence, and a conformance procedure that turns these controls into a concrete check for kernel interchangeability.

</details>


### [33] [CutClean: Neural Network Pruning for Privacy-Preserving Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13773)
*Leonardo Magliolo,Vito Paolo Pastore,Giuseppe Valenzise,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CutClean，一种隐私感知的剪枝方法，通过在网络各模块放置辅助线性隐私头来量化隐私信息泄漏，并通过逐步增加稀疏度来移除私有属性泄漏，在提升网络稀疏性的同时保护敏感属性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在高风险应用中广泛部署，隐私泄露问题日益严重。现有工作关注数据集偏差导致的隐私泄露，但作者指出即使在不存在表征不平衡、无传统数据集偏差的情况下，隐私泄露依然可能发生，这构成了显著的隐私风险。

Method: CutClean采用两个关键设计：(1) 在网络每个模块（block）处放置辅助线性隐私头，用于量化各层的信息泄漏程度；(2) 应用不断提升的稀疏度水平来剪枝网络，以移除最后一个模块上隐私头的准确性所反映的私有属性泄漏，同时保持分类目标任务的准确性。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，CutClean能够有效最小化私有信息的流动，同时实现高稀疏率并保持分类目标任务的准确性。

Conclusion: CutClean证明了通过隐私感知的剪枝方法，可以在增大网络稀疏性的同时有效降低隐私信息泄漏，为高隐私风险场景下部署神经网络提供了一种有效且可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications with growing privacy leakage concerns. We show that this privacy leakage can occur even in the absence of representation imbalances that lead to traditional dataset biases. This poses significant privacy risks when deploying models that process sensitive attributes. In this context, we propose CutClean, a privacy-aware pruning method that allows to reduce privacy information flow through the network, while increasing its sparsity. Our approach employs auxiliary linear privacy heads placed at each network's block to quantify information leakage, and further applies increasing levels of sparsity to remove the private attribute leakage, measured in terms of the accuracy of the privacy head attached to the last block. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach effectively minimizes private information flow while achieving high sparsity rates and preserving classification target accuracy.

</details>


### [34] [PPAPlace: Differentiable Cross-Stage Objectives for Chip Placement Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13790)
*Ruogu Chen,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPAPlace是一个时序驱动的可微代理模型，从宏单元和标准单元布局中预测布线后的PPA（性能、功耗、面积），并通过端到端梯度指导布局优化。相比现有AI布局器仅用HPWL或布线前指标作为训练标签，PPAPlace使用全局布线后的WNS/TNS标签训练双流预测器（图注意力+空间卷积），并通过协同优化和布局后精调两种方式改善布局。在五个未见测试电路上，WNS和TNS平均改善22%和51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宏布局方法主要优化HPWL（半周长线长），但已有基准测试表明HPWL与布线后时序指标（WNS/TNS）的相关性接近于零，导致六个评估的AI布局器相对分层基线均出现PPA退化。虽然已有工作尝试训练跨阶段预测器来弥合这一差距，但现有方法仅使用宏单元表示，且以布线前指标作为训练标签，无法准确反映最终布线后的时序排名。因此需要一种能够从布局直接预测布线后PPA的可微代理，用于时序驱动的布局优化。

Method: PPAPlace包含三个关键组成部分：（1）标签保真度研究——对十个电路在四个设计流程阶段进行实证分析，发现全局布线后时序标签在最终时序保真度和标签生成成本之间达到最佳平衡；（2）双流预测器——结合芯片网表上的图注意力机制和布局网格上的空间卷积，从宏单元和标准单元的布局中预测WNS和TNS；（3）两种梯度利用方式——PPAPlace-CoOpt将预测的WNS/TNS梯度作为协同目标注入分析布局器的优化循环，PPAPlace-Refine则通过投影梯度下降进行布局后宏位置精调。

Result: 在五个未参与训练的ChiPBench测试电路上，PPAPlace相比分层基线平均改善了WNS达22%、TNS达51%，同时保持了功耗和可布线性。该预测器无需针对测试电路重新训练即可实现上述提升。

Conclusion: PPAPlace证明使用全局布线后的时序标签训练可微时序代理，能够有效弥合布局阶段与布线后PPA之间的差距，使AI布局器真正以时序为导向而非单纯优化HPWL，在不牺牲功耗和可布线性的前提下显著改善布线后时序。

Abstract: Macro placement significantly affects a chip's post-route performance, power, and area (PPA). Most placement methods optimize half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) as the primary objective. However, recent benchmarking shows a near-zero correlation between HPWL and post-route timing metrics such as the worst negative slack (WNS) and total negative slack (TNS). As a result, all six evaluated artificial intelligence (AI) placers degraded PPA relative to the hierarchical baseline. Recent efforts have tried to train cross-stage predictors to close this gap. However, existing methods focus on macro-only representations and use pre-route metrics as training labels. A label fidelity study of ten circuits at four design flow stages reveals that HPWL and pre-route timing poorly reflect final post-route timing rankings. In contrast, post-global-routing achieves the best balance between final timing fidelity and label generation cost-effectiveness. Based on this finding, PPAPlace is a timing-driven differentiable surrogate predicting post-route PPA from macro and standard-cell placements. The surrogate is a dual-stream predictor that combines graph attention over the chip netlist with spatial convolution over the placement grid. It is trained on post-global-routing labels. The predicted WNS and TNS gradients flow end-to-end back to cell coordinates. PPAPlace exploits these gradients in two ways: as a co-objective injected into an analytical placer's optimization loop (PPAPlace-CoOpt), and as a post-placement refinement step that adjusts macro positions via projected gradient descent (PPAPlace-Refine). On five ChiPBench test circuits excluded from training, PPAPlace improves average WNS and TNS by 22\% and 51\% over the hierarchical baseline while preserving power and routability, using the same predictor without test-circuit retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/ValleyC/PPAPlace.

</details>


### [35] [Recent Advances in Deep Learning-Based Drug-Target Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13797)
*Jafin Khan,Md Hossain Shuvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对药物-靶标结合亲和力预测的近期机器学习方法进行了全面综述和比较分析，涵盖七种常用基准数据集和评估指标，揭示了数据集偏差、冷启动泛化能力不足等问题，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习在药物-靶标结合亲和力预测领域取得了进展，但准确预测仍是一个开放的研究问题。本文旨在通过系统综述和比较分析，识别现有方法的优势、局限性和研究空白，推动该领域的发展。

Method: 作者综述了代表性的深度学习方法（涵盖多种神经网络架构和表征策略），分析了七种广泛使用的基准数据集和常用评估指标，并进行比较分析以评估各方法在标准基准和冷启动场景下的表现。

Result: 分析表明，虽然许多方法在标准基准上报告了强性能，但其有效性常受数据集偏差和有限评估设置的影响；大多数方法在冷启动场景下表现下降，揭示了泛化挑战。主要局限包括数据集不平衡、缺乏标准化评估、真实世界适用性有限以及冷启动问题。

Conclusion: 本文提出未来研究方向包括：更好的数据集设计、更稳健的评估方法、改善冷启动问题处理，以及整合多模态表征。

Abstract: Computational approaches to drug discovery involve multiple sub-problems, and among them, drug-target binding affinity prediction plays an important role. Despite recent advances, accurately predicting binding affinity remains an open research area. The major objective of our paper is to perform a comprehensive review and comparative analysis of recent machine learning methods for drug-target binding affinity prediction, with a focus on identifying strengths, limitations, and research gaps. We review representative recent deep learning approaches that use common benchmark datasets and evaluation metrics, covering a range of neural network architectures and representation strategies. In addition, we analyze seven widely used benchmark datasets and commonly adopted evaluation metrics for drug-target binding affinity prediction. Our analysis indicates that although many methods report strong performance on standard benchmarks, their effectiveness is often influenced by dataset bias and limited evaluation settings. Furthermore, most methods exhibit reduced performance in cold-start scenarios, highlighting challenges in generalization. We identify several limitations of current approaches, including dataset imbalance, the lack of standardized evaluation, limited real-world applicability, and challenges in cold-start scenarios. We also discuss future research directions, including better dataset design, more robust evaluation methods, improved handling of cold-start problems, and the integration of multimodal representations.

Abstract: This paper presents an event-driven learning and benchmarking framework for the Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problem with progressively revealed requests and evolving vehicle states. Masked MLP and Transformer policies are trained through behavior cloning and proximal policy optimization. Deterministic feasibility masking prevents invalid vehicle--request assignments, while fixed-prefix/flexible-suffix route commitments protect completed, active, and near-term decisions and separately measure vehicle reassignment and resequencing. The learned policies are compared with dynamic insertion heuristics and time-limited rolling-horizon optimization. In a 20-scenario policy benchmark, all methods completed every request without invalid actions, but nearest feasible achieved the lowest mean objective and outperformed the learned policies in routing quality, waiting time, stability, makespan, and runtime. Across five independent training runs, PPO had little average effect on the MLP and improved the Transformer on average, although with greater seed variability. Under the common protocol, nearest feasible achieved the lowest combined objective and route disruption, whereas rolling horizon achieved the lowest waiting times and makespan at substantially higher computational cost. The learned policies retained millisecond-level decisions and transferred to instances with up to 80 requests without retraining, but did not outperform the strongest heuristic. No single method was best across routing efficiency, service responsiveness, stability, and online computation.

</details>


### [37] [Stochastic Control Policies for Robust Molecular Transition Path Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13800)
*Jingqian Liu,Yu-Hsiang Wang,Yanru Qu,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于随机策略的转换路径采样（TPS）方法——FS-TPS和LaS-TPS，通过在控制策略中引入随机性来改善rollout-based控制方法的稳定性和种子依赖性，在三个生物分子系统上显著提升了转换成功率和路径质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于rollout的控制力学习方法在TPS中虽然能产生更物理合理的轨迹，但存在性能不稳定、对随机初始化高度敏感的问题。作者将rollout-based控制重新理解为路径空间上的提议分布学习，并探讨随机性放置作为一种设计选择，以改善探索能力和优化鲁棒性。

Method: 将rollout-based控制方法形式化为学习路径空间上的提议分布，提出两种随机策略：(1) FS-TPS，直接在控制策略输出上参数化状态相关的Gaussian分布；(2) LaS-TPS，采样一个紧凑的潜在控制变量并将其解码为结构化、跨原子相关的力变化。

Result: 在三个规模递增的生物分子系统（丙氨酸二肽、chignolin、BBL快速折叠蛋白）上进行多种子实验，随机策略相比确定性策略基线在转换成功率和路径质量上均有一致提升，同时显著降低了对随机初始化的敏感性。

Conclusion: 在控制策略中引入随机性能够有效提升rollout-based TPS方法的鲁棒性和采样质量，为设计更稳定高效的生物分子转换路径采样方法提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Transition path sampling (TPS) aims to efficiently generate rare molecular transition trajectories between metastable states and is essential for understanding biomolecular mechanisms. Beyond traditional molecular dynamics (MD)-based sampling, machine learning has become central to state-of-the-art TPS. One major class of methods learns control forces during explicit MD rollouts. By preserving the underlying molecular dynamics, these methods tend to produce more physically plausible trajectories than endpoint-conditioned generators that construct paths directly. However, rollout-based control methods have been reported to exhibit unstable and strongly seed-dependent performance. We recast rollout-based control as learning a path-space proposal distribution and investigate stochasticity placement as a design choice for improving exploration and optimization robustness. We develop two stochastic policies: FS-TPS, which directly parameterizes a state-dependent Gaussian distribution over the control policy output, and LaS-TPS, which samples a compact latent control variable and decodes it into structured, cross-atom-correlated force variation. We conduct extensive multi-seed experiments on three biomolecular systems of increasing size: alanine dipeptide, chignolin, and BBL, a fast-folding protein. Stochastic policies consistently improve transition success and path quality over deterministic-policy baselines while substantially reducing sensitivity to random initialization.

</details>


### [38] [HI-MeshGraphNets: Efficient and Accurate Mesh-based Physics Learning with Hierarchical Multi-scale Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13827)
*SiHun Lee,Dong-Hyuk Park,Taesoo Bang,Seung-Hoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HI-MGN，一种基于层次化多尺度图神经网络的物理代理模型，通过最远点采样和Voronoi划分对网格进行粗化，在粗图上进行消息传递以高效传播长程信息，再通过学习到的插值网络重建细粒度特征，在结构和流体基准上相比现有方法提升了精度并降低了训练时间和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的平面消息传递图神经网络在处理大规模高保真网格时效率低下，因为信息每层只能传播一跳，需要深层网络才能覆盖长程交互，导致计算成本、内存占用和过平滑风险增加。

Method: HI-MGN通过以下方式实现多尺度消息传递：1) 使用最远点采样和Voronoi划分对原始网格图进行层次化粗化，保留原始网格拓扑；2) 在粗图上进行消息传递，使信息在更少的层数内跨越更大的几何距离；3) 使用学习得到的图插值网络将粗尺度特征重建为细尺度分辨率，替代MeshGraphNets中的平面处理器。

Result: 在三个结构和流体基准测试中，HI-MGN相比MeshGraphNets和Bi-Stride Multi-Scale GNN取得了更高的精度，同时减少了训练时间和峰值内存使用。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的层次化消息传递结合学习到的粗到细插值方法，为可扩展的基于网格的物理代理建模提供了一个有效且实用的框架。

Abstract: Machine-learned physical surrogate models have become promising alternatives to mesh-based numerical solvers. Among them, graph neural networks (GNNs) are well suited for representing simulation meshes and learning nodal state evolution through message passing. However, conventional flat message passing becomes inefficient on large, high-fidelity meshes because information propagates only one hop per layer, requiring deep processors for long-range interactions and increasing computational cost, memory usage, and the risk of over-smoothing.
  To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Interpolating MeshGraphNets (HI-MGN), a multiscale extension of MeshGraphNets for efficient long-range communication on unstructured meshes. HI-MGN replaces the flat processor with a hierarchical multiscale processor that coarsens graphs using farthest-point sampling and Voronoi partitioning while preserving the original mesh topology. Message passing on coarse graphs enables information to travel over larger geometric distances with fewer layers, and a learned graph interpolation network reconstructs fine-resolution features.
  Across three structural and fluid benchmarks, HI-MGN achieves improved accuracy compared with MeshGraphNets and the Bi-Stride Multi-Scale GNN while reducing training time and peak memory usage. The results show that topology-aware hierarchical message passing and learned coarse-to-fine interpolation provide an effective and practical framework for scalable mesh-based physics surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [39] [Federated Prompt Learning: A Unified Framework, Empirical Analysis, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13844)
*Qinglin Yang,Chen Qiu,Hongyuan Zhang,Pengdeng Li,Yuan Liu,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦提示学习（FPL）进行了全面综述，系统整合了联邦学习范式与大型语言模型，围绕动机、特性、权衡、安全隐私挑战及未来方向三个研究问题展开，覆盖了从预训练到微调及实际应用的全模型生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已在学术界和工业界成为云端智能服务的核心组件，但其训练和部署面临高昂计算成本、数据集中化以及隐私泄露风险。联邦学习提供去中心化训练范式，可在不共享原始数据的情况下协作训练模型，是隐私保护LLM训练与推理的有前景方案，因此需要系统梳理FL与LLM结合的最新进展。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，围绕三个研究问题展开：(1)梳理FPL的基本动机、特性、使能技术及其与传统联邦学习和全模型联邦微调的区别；(2)分析FPL方法在性能、通信效率、计算开销、可扩展性、个性化和异构性处理方面的权衡；(3)考察剩余的安全、隐私、鲁棒性和系统挑战。综述覆盖模型全生命周期（预训练、微调、实际应用），并总结现有防御机制。

Result: 系统性地检视了现有FPL方法在预训练、微调和实际应用中的进展，讨论了安全、隐私和鲁棒性问题，总结归纳了现有防御机制，并识别了各维度上的关键权衡。

Conclusion: 指出FPL领域仍存在诸多开放挑战和未来研究方向，旨在帮助读者理解洞察如何驱动FPL的后续研究发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become core components of cloud-based intelligent services in academia and industry, yet their training and deployment are hindered by high computational costs, data centralization, and privacy concerns. Federated learning (FL) offers a decentralized training paradigm that enables clients to collaboratively train a learning model without sharing raw data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving LLM training and reasoning. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of federated prompt learning (FPL) to review recent advances in integrating the federated learning paradigm and large language models, answering the following research questions: RQ1: The fundamental motivations, characteristics, and enabling technologies of FPL, and how it differs from conventional FL and full-model federated fine-tuning; RQ2: The trade-offs FPL approaches exhibit in performance, communication efficiency, computational overhead, scalability, personalization, and heterogeneity handling; RQ3: The remaining security, privacy, robustness, and system challenges, along with key future research directions. To this end, we systematically examine existing FPL methods across the full model lifecycle: pre-training, fine-tuning, and practical applications, while discussing security, privacy, and robustness issues and summarizing existing defense mechanisms. Finally, we highlight open challenges and future directions, aiming to help readers understand how the insights drive research in FPL.

</details>

*Benjamín Schindler,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何过滤的框架，通过句子嵌入空间中与真实类示例的欧氏距离来筛选LLM生成的合成训练数据，并采用软加权机制将过滤分数转化为样本权重用于分类器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的合成训练数据质量参差不齐：有些样本落在正确的类区域，而有些则位于边缘或跨类区域，直接使用这些低质量样本会影响分类器性能。

Method: 在句子嵌入空间中计算每个LLM生成样本到真实类示例的欧氏距离，筛选几何上一致的候选样本；通过软加权机制将过滤分数转换为样本权重用于分类器训练。

Result: 在13个数据集、5个分类器、10种增强方法和6700多个配置上，比SMOTE提升+2.61个百分点（p<0.0001，Cohen's d=0.95，88.9%胜率）。在命名实体识别任务上提升+9.26pp，100%胜率，且无需修改过滤条件。对来自4个提供商的5种LLM均表现稳健。关键发现：最简单的基于距离的过滤器始终优于复杂的多标准替代方案。

Conclusion: 几何过滤框架是提升LLM合成训练数据质量的有效且通用的方法，简单距离度量优于复杂方案，且可跨任务、跨模型泛化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate synthetic training data for text classification, but the quality of generated samples is heterogeneous: some fall in correct class regions of the embedding space while others land in peripheral or cross-class zones. We propose a geometric filtering framework that evaluates each LLM-generated sample by its Euclidean distance to real class examples in a sentence embedding space, selecting only geometrically consistent candidates. A soft weighting mechanism transforms filter scores into sample weights for classifier training. Evaluated across 13 datasets, 5 classifiers, 10 augmentation methods, and over 6,700 configurations, our method achieves +2.61 percentage points (pp) over SMOTE ($p<0.0001$, Cohen's $d=0.95$, 88.9% win rate). The approach generalizes to named entity recognition (+9.26pp, 100% win rate) without filter modification, and is robust across 5 LLMs from 4 providers. A key finding is that the simplest distance-based filter consistently outperforms complex multi-criteria alternatives.

</details>


### [41] [Variation Brownian Kernel Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13882)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变分布朗核阶梯（VBKL），一个路径原子函数空间框架，将非线性递归字典构建与线性变分叠加分离，并系统建立了该空间的深度增长理论、正则性保证、泛化界限及近似构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的优势声称高度依赖于如何衡量表示的复杂度，缺乏一个统一的函数空间框架来阐明深度带来的严格收益。本文将"深度收益归因于复杂度定义"这一核心问题置于函数分析框架下，从而严格刻画深度的价值。

Method: 从线性投影出发，每个原子从布朗再生核希尔伯特空间递归组合单位球轮廓；完整VBKL空间为完成字典的带符号测度变分包。将每个递归字典识别为布朗回拉RKHS球的并集，并用布朗二次混沌、带符号阈值迹和VC熵推导Rademacher与泛化界限；再通过离散化外测度和外层布朗轮廓构造两阶段逼近器。

Result: 建立了变分控制的Hölder正则性、紧性与可达性，证明在局部非退化条件下深度严格增长；获得M^-1/2+m^-1/2的误差界、最优插值常数√(A/2)、每次求值至多2M个活跃外层基贡献的稀疏性，以及Rademacher与泛化界限。实验验证了逼近机制并展示有利的有限数据精度-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: VBKL提供了一个严谨的函数空间框架，从理论上厘清了深度在表示学习中的严格收益条件，在有限数据场景下展现出良好的精度与复杂度平衡，为深度架构的可解释性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Claims about the benefit of depth depend on the complexity assigned to a representation. We introduce the \emph{Variation Brownian Kernel Ladder} (VBKL), a path-atomic function-space framework that separates nonlinear recursive dictionary construction from linear variation superposition. Starting from linear projections, each atom recursively composes unit-ball profiles from the Brownian reproducing kernel Hilbert space; the full VBKL space is then the signed-measure variation hull of the completed dictionary. We identify each recursive dictionary as a union of Brownian pullback RKHS balls and establish variation-controlled Hölder regularity, compactness and attainment, and strict growth with depth under a local non-degeneracy condition whose trace lies in the support of the input measure. For associated finite lower-support architectures, we derive Rademacher and generalization bounds through Brownian quadratic chaos, signed threshold traces, and VC entropy. We also construct two-stage approximants by discretizing the outer measure and the selected outer Brownian profiles, obtaining an $M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}$ error bound, a sharp interpolation constant $\sqrt{A/2}$, and at most $2M$ active outer-profile basis contributions per evaluation. Controlled experiments illustrate the approximation mechanisms and indicate a favorable limited-data accuracy--complexity trade-off.

</details>


### [42] [Fashion Outfit Generation via Unified Sequential Composition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13888)
*Kaicheng Pang,Xingxing Zou,Ruohan Xu,Waikeung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将时尚穿搭生成任务形式化为约束集合生成（CEG），并建模为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程，通过统一序列组合模型（USCM）联合建模集合级兼容性与潜在组合意图，配合潜在扩展蒙特卡洛树搜索（LE-MCTS）进行物品检索，在多个数据集上实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚穿搭生成任务的核心挑战在于审美兼容性的非单调性和隐含性，以及指数级组合搜索空间。传统方法难以有效处理大规模物品库中的风格协调穿搭合成问题，需要一种能够捕捉集合级兼容性并高效搜索的组合建模方法。

Method: 1) 将时尚穿搭生成形式化为约束集合生成（CEG），建模为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程；2) 提出统一序列组合模型（USCM），联合建模集合级兼容性与潜在组合意图；3) 提出潜在扩展蒙特卡洛树搜索（LE-MCTS）机制，在USCM学习先验指导下平衡局部审美协同与全局结构平衡的物品检索与组合。

Result: 在Polyvore Outfits数据集上进行了广泛实验，并在iFashion和PolyvoreU数据集上进行了零样本评估，在独立人工偏好评估、自动化审美代理指标和结构有效性指标方面均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的USCM+LE-MCTS框架有效解决了约束条件下的时尚穿搭生成问题，通过联合建模集合级兼容性和潜在意图，并利用树搜索机制实现高效组合，在多种评估维度上均展现了优越性。

Abstract: The task of synthesizing stylistically coherent fashion outfits from massive item libraries, known as fashion outfit generation, remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the non-monotonic and implicit nature of aesthetic compatibility, coupled with the exponentially large combinatorial search space. In this paper, we formalize this task as Constrained Ensemble Generation (CEG) and model it as a finite-horizon deterministic Markov Decision Process. To address CEG in fashion, we propose the Unified Sequential Composition Model (USCM), which jointly models set-level compatibility and latent composition intents. Guided by USCM's learned priors, a Latent Expansion Monte Carlo Tree Search (LE-MCTS) mechanism is proposed to handle item retrieval during composition, balancing local aesthetic synergy with global structural balance. Extensive experiments on the Polyvore Outfits dataset, along with zero-shot evaluations on the iFashion and PolyvoreU datasets, demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance across independent human preference evaluations, automated aesthetic proxies, and structural validity metrics for constrained fashion outfit generation.

</details>


### [43] [MedMix: Specialization-Consistent Federated Sparse MoEs under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13911)
*Adiba Orzikulova,Dong Min Kim,Jaehong Yoon,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MedMix，一个面向联邦多模态稀疏专家混合(MoE)模型的语义对齐框架，解决跨客户端模态异质性导致的专家路由分歧和不兼容特化问题，通过模态上下文感知路由、共识引导的路由对齐和客户端自适应专家聚合三个机制，在真实医疗多模态数据集上取得了最佳平均F1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦多模态医疗AI面临客户端层面和样本层面的模态异质性：不同客户端可能系统性缺失某些模态类型，同一客户端内的个体记录也可能包含不同的部分模态子集。稀疏MoE虽可通过模态自适应计算缓解这一问题，但在跨客户端模态异质性下，本地学习到的路由策略可能在不同客户端间产生分歧，导致标准的联邦聚合无法对齐或覆盖专家特化，破坏了稀疏MoE的设计初衷。

Method: MedMix包含三个核心组件：(1) 客户端侧采用模态上下文感知路由，利用token的模态身份、位置和不完整度上下文指导专家选择；(2) 跨客户端侧采用共识引导的路由对齐，在服务端为共享模态模式构建共识锚点并对齐各客户端的本地路由分布；(3) 客户端自适应专家聚合，利用客户端特定的模态模式原型匹配并聚合功能相似的专家。

Result: 在真实世界多模态医疗数据集上的实验表明，MedMix在多种模态异质性和模态不完整性设置下取得了最佳平均F1分数，在严重异质性场景下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: MedMix通过语义对齐框架有效协调了联邦多模态稀疏MoE中跨客户端的路由和专家特化，验证了其在严峻模态异质性条件下的鲁棒性和优越性。

Abstract: Federated multimodal medical AI faces modality heterogeneity at both the client and sample levels: clients may systematically lack access to specific modality types, while individual records within the same client may contain different partial modality subsets. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are a promising remedy for modality-adaptive computation, but their use in federated learning is fragile under cross-client modality heterogeneity, where locally learned routing policies can diverge across clients and drive experts toward incompatible specializations. Different clients may assign the same observed modality configuration to different experts, or train similarly indexed experts on different missing-modality configurations, causing standard aggregation to misalign or overwrite the expert specialization that sparse MoEs are intended to learn. To address this challenge, we propose MedMix, a semantic-alignment framework for federated multimodal sparse MoEs that coordinates cross-client routing and expert specialization using modality context. At the client side, MedMix uses modality-context-aware routing to guide expert selection using each token's modality identity, position, and incompleteness context. Across clients, it uses consensus-guided routing alignment to construct server-side consensus anchors for shared modality patterns and align local routing distributions across clients. Complementing these routing mechanisms, client-adaptive expert aggregation leverages client-specific modality-pattern prototypes to match and aggregate functionally similar experts across clients. Experiments on real-world multimodal medical datasets show that MedMix achieves the best average F1 across diverse modality heterogeneity and modality incompleteness settings, with especially clear gains under severe heterogeneity.

</details>
