We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [79] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机权重平均（SWA）作为一种无需重复训练的数据增强替代方案，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练轨迹，证明在无限宽度极限下SWA在增强数据上能提供超越常规性能提升的等变性增强，并通过大量数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强是向神经网络引入对称性的简单有效方式，但无限深度集成虽然能实现对称性却需要重复训练多次，成本高昂。本文旨在寻找一种无需重复训练的替代方法，即随机权重平均（SWA），来获得类似的对称性收益。

Method: 本文将训练后期（end of training）的随机训练轨迹近似为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（OU）过程，在此框架下分析SWA的行为。在无限宽度极限下，证明SWA结合数据增强能够提供等变性（equiviariance）提升。

Result: 理论分析表明，SWA在增强数据上产生的等变性提升超出了仅由SWA带来的性能提升本身所能解释的范畴。在计算机视觉和图分类的多个模型上进行了广泛数值实验，涵盖离散和连续对称性，结果验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: SWA是一种在不需要重复训练的前提下获得对称性等变性收益的有效方法，为深度学习中利用对称性提供了更经济的替代方案。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [80] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeaMoE是一种面向低延迟解码场景的MoE架构改进方法，通过将专家按专业领域分组并共享大部分参数、每个专家仅保留少量私有参数，并设计两阶段路由策略避免冗余权重加载，从而显著降低LLM解码时的参数加载量，提升端到端推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型广泛应用于实时交互场景（如编程助手、实时音视频交互），这些场景对响应延迟要求极低，通常采用小批量解码。然而小批量解码下MoE推理变为内存受限，专家权重加载成为严重瓶颈。现有解决方案（如训练后权重压缩、预训练时的细粒度专家设计）要么降低模型精度，要么引入额外计算和通信开销，因此亟需一种解码高效的MoE架构。

Method: DeaMoE将专家划分为多个"部门"（departments），同一部门的专家来自相同专业领域并共享大部分参数，同时每个专家保留少量私有参数以体现其独特性。此外，设计了定制的两阶段路由策略，避免冗余参数加载，从而在LLM解码阶段大幅提升效率。

Result: 与标准MoE相比，DeaMoE在预训练7B模型上（A40 GPU）将每步加载权重减少最多50.9%，端到端TPOT加速最高达1.33倍；在微基准测试中，DeepSeek-V3在A40和H100上分别获得最高2.00倍和1.97倍的峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过部门化参数共享与两阶段路由策略，在不损失模型精度的前提下显著缓解了小批量解码下MoE的权重加载瓶颈，为低延迟实时交互场景提供了一种高效的MoE架构解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [81] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CytoBERT，一个面向单细胞流式细胞术数据（CyTOF/FACS）的开源基础模型。通过自监督预训练于包含50M+细胞、15个异质人类数据集的大规模语料库，该模型能够处理可变标记面板（不同实验设定的不同抗体组合），并展示了下游样本级分类中跨数据集迁移学习的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式细胞术数据在免疫学和临床中广泛应用，但高度异质且缺乏标准化（不同实验方案和特征选择），导致传统机器学习方法难以跨研究迁移。虽然基础模型在多个领域取得了突破，但在细胞计数数据分析中相关方法仍然稀缺，亟需一个能处理可变标记面板的通用基础模型。

Method: CytoBERT采用自监督预训练策略，在大规模细胞计数语料库上训练。该语料库包含15个人类数据集，涵盖异质标记面板和超过5000万个细胞，并通过标记标准化（marker standardization）进行整理，使模型能够学习细胞内部可迁移的标记间关系。预训练后，通过微调执行样本级分类任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，在多个数据集上对CytoBERT进行微调后，跨异质细胞计数数据集的迁移学习是可行的，模型能够有效学习可迁移的标记间关系，为可扩展、可泛化的细胞计数分析奠定了基础。

Conclusion: CytoBERT作为首个面向可变标记面板单细胞计数数据的开源基础模型，验证了基础模型范式在细胞计数领域的适用性，为后续大规模、通用化的流式数据分析和跨研究整合提供了可行的起点和开源基础设施。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [82] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Power Sampling在推理时锐化语言模型分布虽能提升单样本正确性，却会意外损害下游推理（如自一致性），准确率最多下降18.5个百分点。作者将悖论归因于剂量失配与覆盖失配，并提出一种形变控制、支持保持的Power目标修复该问题，在同等预算下优于标准多样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling无需验证器即可在推理时通过锐化完整生成轨迹分布来提升推理能力，并有潜力作为下游采样方法的通用前端。但作者发现一个悖论：它可能在推动更多概率质量朝向正确轨迹的同时，反而削弱其旨在增强的下游推理能力，这一矛盾亟需探究和解决。

Method: 作者以自一致性为代表性案例进行实证分析，识别出两个失配来源：（1）剂量失配——固定指数在不同问题上引发截然不同的分布变化量；（2）覆盖失配——全局锐化将质量集中到少数主导路径，即使pass@k高（常被解读为多样性保留）也会丧失下游聚合、搜索和选择所需的广泛推理路径支持。基于此诊断，作者用形变控制、支持保持的Power目标替代统一轨迹幂指数化，跨问题校准锐化程度并限制对中等概率路径的抑制。

Result: 在多种模型和推理基准上，全局Power Sampling导致自一致性准确率最多下降18.5个百分点。修复后的采样器在同等预算的加权自一致性设置中，逆转了全局Power造成的损失，并在多个推理基准上优于标准多样本推理。

Conclusion: Power Sampling的全局锐化存在根本性缺陷：高pass@k可能掩盖推理路径多样性的实际丧失。只有通过跨问题自适应校准锐化程度并保持支撑集广度，才能使Power Sampling真正成为通用的下游推理采样前端，实现比标准多样本推理更优的性能。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [83] [Designing Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models: A Unified Path-Space View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14430)
*Yixian Xu,Yuanrui Zhang,Shengjie Luo,Liwei Wang,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从路径空间原理出发，统一了扩散模型的后训练强化学习（RL）算法，证明了反向轨迹方法和前向匹配方法源自同一个正则化扩散RL目标，并将它们之间的经验差距归结为方差缩减效应而非RL原则差异，进而提出了一种多样本KDE值梯度估计器及尺度有界权重族，在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image模型上验证了方差缩减解释并超越了现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型的RL后训练算法呈现碎片化状态：反向轨迹方法依赖离散化的似然比，而前向匹配方法则在带奖励标签的噪声化样本上训练。现有研究缺乏一个统一的理论框架来理解这些看似不同方法之间的关系，导致算法设计缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 从正则化扩散RL目标出发，利用采样SDE之间的重要性采样获得轨迹空间上的显式策略梯度估计器。该估计器包含Flow-GRPO类更新中的随机Itô积分；通过推导等价的方差缩减值梯度形式，恢复AWM和DiffusionNFT的前向匹配结构。在此基础上提出统一的设计空间（按值梯度估计、权重函数和采样选择组织），并提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器和尺度有界权重族。

Result: 在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image模型上的实验验证了"方法族间差异源于方差缩减效应"的解释，所提出的算法配方在扩散RL基线上取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文统一了扩散模型RL后训练的两大类方法，揭示了它们共同的路径空间原理，将经验差异重新定义为方差缩减效应，并提供了一套统一的设计空间指导后续算法的构建与改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training provides a direct way to align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific rewards. However, current RL algorithms for diffusion models remain fragmented: reverse-trajectory methods rely on discretized likelihood ratios, whereas forward-matching methods train on reward-labeled noising versions of the rollout samples. This paper shows that these seemingly different losses arise from a single path-space principle. Starting from the regularized diffusion-RL objective, we use importance sampling between sampling SDEs to obtain an explicit policy-gradient estimator on trajectory space. The estimator contains the stochastic Itô integral underlying Flow-GRPO-type updates; we derive an equivalent variance-reduced value-gradient form that recovers the forward-matching structure of AWM and DiffusionNFT. This identifies the empirical gap between these method families as a variance-reduction effect rather than a difference in RL principle. The derivation yields a unified design space organized by value-gradient estimation, weight functions, and sampling choices. Within this space, we propose a multi-sample KDE value-gradient estimator that reuses rollout groups, together with scale-bounded weight families that retain stable existing recipes while excluding singular ones. Experiments on SD3.5-M and Qwen-Image models validate the variance-reduction explanation and show that the resulting recipe improves over prior diffusion-RL baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用双层优化框架用于神经架构搜索（NAS），通过三种连续松弛方案（神经元门控NAS-NG、混合激活NAS-MA及两者结合NAS-NGMA）替代离散的神经元和激活层决策，在MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多种架构上实现可微优化。实验表明该方法能在MNIST和CIFAR-10上找到紧凑且性能优异的架构，并优于DARTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS天然是双层优化问题（上层优化架构、下层训练参数），但离散的架构决策、指数级增长的搜索空间以及候选架构的高训练成本导致其计算开销巨大。

Method: 提出三种可扩展的连续松弛公式化方法将离散的神经元级和激活级决策转化为可微优化问题，派生三种NAS方法：基于神经元门控的NAS-NG、基于混合激活的NAS-MA，以及两者结合的NAS-NGMA，适用于MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多种架构。

Result: 在MNIST上，NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%测试准确率；NAS-NG以仅0.26M CNN参数达到99.63%准确率。在CIFAR-10上持续优于vanilla DARTS。此外NAS-NG能优化过度参数化和文献最优架构，在减少参数的同时提升准确率。

Conclusion: 松弛双层优化可作为离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为高效神经元级和激活级架构优化提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [85] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的微分神经架构搜索（NAS）框架，利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构建线性规划来计算架构更新方向，在保持模型参数最优性的同时提升泛化能力。该方法在DARTS搜索空间基础上衍生出S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS两种变体，在CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100上超越标准DARTS及其多个变体，并在ImageNet上验证了架构的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分NAS通过将架构搜索空间松弛到连续域，使得连续优化原理可以应用于NAS，但现有微分NAS方法在导航架构搜索空间时效率和泛化能力仍有提升空间。作者希望通过数学规划（尤其是线性规划）来更高效、更有效地进行架构优化。

Method: LP-NAS将微分NAS问题形式化为线性规划：利用验证损失梯度（提供泛化方向）和训练损失Hessian（保证模型参数最优性）构造LP，求解得到架构更新方向。作者引入两种计算高效的变体——S-LP-NAS（稀疏变体）和R-LP-NAS（正则化变体），并将其嵌入DARTS搜索空间形成S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，LP-DARTS在架构搜索和评估阶段均优于标准DARTS，且在早期搜索迭代中收敛更快、验证性能显著更高。与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS、STO-DARTS等DARTS变体在CIFAR-10上的对比也验证了其有效性，并通过ImageNet实验确认了所发现架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: LP-NAS证明了数学规划（特别是线性规划）在微分NAS中的有效性和实用性，为NAS提供了新的优化视角，不仅提升了搜索效率还改善了泛化性能，且所发现的架构具有良好的跨数据集迁移能力。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [86] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了sMuon（small Muon）优化器，通过线性化和最小二乘近似，在低秩参数化下近似求解松弛的Muon目标，使得Muon优化器能够与LoRA等参数高效微调（PEFT）方法兼容使用。实验表明sMuon在SFT和ReLoRA预训练中表现良好，整体上为低秩微调带来适度的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在神经网络预训练中比传统优化器（如Adam等）有显著优势，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中较少使用。根本原因是：最常见的PEFT方法LoRA采用低秩参数化，而Muon需要对权重更新进行正交化，这在数学上无法直接应用于低秩结构。作者旨在解决这一数学障碍，使Muon能够惠及低秩微调场景。

Method: 作者通过两个关键步骤解决低秩条件下的Muon难题：(1) 线性化——对Muon的更新目标在低秩设定下进行近似简化；(2) 最小二乘——利用最小二乘法求解松弛后的Muon目标。实现上仅使用矩阵乘法（matmul）运算，避免了复杂的线性代数分解（如SVD等）例程，从而保证了计算效率。

Result: sMuon在监督微调（SFT）和ReLoRA预训练实验中均表现良好。尽管具体效果因模型和评估任务而异，但总体上，将Muon用于低秩微调带来了适度的性能改进。

Conclusion: sMuon成功弥合了Muon优化器与LoRA低秩参数化之间的理论鸿沟，提供了一种高效、易实现且仅基于矩阵乘法运算的方案，使Muon的优势能够扩展到参数高效微调领域，为低秩微调场景带来了实用的性能增益。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [87] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段跨表格数据生成框架（CTDG），首先将多个异构原始表格转换为具有相同列集合的标准化统计表，再使用扩散Transformer模型学习并生成合成统计表，最后通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建出逼真的合成异构表格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成表格数据生成方法主要局限于单输入表格场景，难以有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异构表格。作者希望突破这一限制，实现从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型并生成无限数量的合成异构表格。

Method: 该方法分为两个阶段：(1) 将每个异构原始表转换为标准化统计表，捕获原始列的边际分布和列间成对相关性；(2) 训练一个扩散Transformer模型学习这些同构统计表间的结构模式并生成合成统计表，随后通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建合成原始表格。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在学习到的统计表示上具有高保真度，在生成合成数据的保真度-多样性权衡方面表现良好，验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架成功克服了传统方法只能处理单表格的限制，能够从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型，并支持生成无限数量的逼真合成异构表格数据。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [88] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rollplex是一个面向视觉-语言模型(VLM)的强化学习(RL)后训练运行时系统，通过将参考/训练阶段中的prefix计算移入rollout解码窗口重叠执行，并采用阶段感知内存管理和并行度感知权重共享机制，在保持同步on-policy语义不变的前提下，实现了1.23x–1.30x（相对于串行共置）和1.57x–2.24x（相对于同GPU预算下的解耦）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy RL运行时将rollout、reference scoring和actor training严格串行执行，这种阶段粒度的执行方式在文本RL中有效，但对于VLM而言会造成GPU计算浪费。因为VLM处理密集视频输入和prompt前缀占用了每个阶段的大量时间，而这些前缀计算独立于生成的响应，本可在rollout解码期间并行执行。此外，VLM模型规模大（如Qwen2.5-VL-32B每GPU需约165GiB），且rollout与训练偏好不同的张量并行度（TP）和权重布局，直接共置存在严重的内存和并行约束。

Method: Rollplex包含两大核心机制：(1) 阶段感知内存管理（Phase-aware memory management）：根据生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留，实现参考和训练阶段分解，将prefix计算移入rollout解码窗口；(2) 并行度感知权重共享（Parallelism-aware weight sharing）：对布局兼容的张量在不同TP度间共享同一物理存储，仅重建不兼容的张量，避免完整的第二份actor副本。Rollplex将参考/训练阶段解构并调度到rollout解码期间并发执行，同时保持同步on-policy更新语义。

Result: 在32块H800 GPU上，Rollplex相较于串行共置实现1.23x–1.30x加速，相较于同GPU预算下的解耦（disaggregation）方案实现1.57x–2.24x加速，同时保持了同步RL更新的约束条件未受影响。

Conclusion: Rollplex证明了通过细粒度的阶段分解和计算重叠调度，可以有效利用VLM RL训练中被prefix处理占据的大规模计算，在不牺牲同步on-policy语义的前提下显著提升GPU利用率和端到端训练速度。其阶段感知内存管理和并行度感知权重共享机制为解决大规模VLM多阶段RL训练的算力和内存瓶颈提供了有效的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [89] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RecipeNet，一种分层Transformer架构，用于处理配方数据（如材料合成、药物配方、工业制造中的有序步骤序列），通过堆叠Transformer编码器同时捕获步骤内字段交互和步骤间序列依赖，在多个配方数据集和任务上优于现有表格模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据的结构化特性（有序步骤序列、异构字段）被现有表格学习方法忽略，后者通常将这种结构扁平化为固定schema表示，无法有效捕获层次化的字段交互和程序性依赖关系。

Method: RecipeNet采用分层Transformer架构：底层Transformer编码器建模每个步骤内部的字段级交互，顶层Transformer编码器建模跨步骤的序列依赖关系，通过堆叠两层编码器实现层次化配方表示学习。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet consistently优于现有表格模型，验证了分层和序列建模对配方表示学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 配方数据能够从分层与序列化的建模方式中显著受益，RecipeNet为配方表示学习提供了一种有效的通用方法。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge通过反射式进化从少量带真实标签的轨迹中自动生成可读的评判标准文本，用于大语言模型agent的自动评判，在tau-bench和WebShop上相比G-Eval更少将失败轨迹误判为成功（false-pass率降低近一半），尽管在总体一致性和分数校准上并无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型agent的规模化评测越来越依赖第二个语言模型作为自动评判器（judge），因为可执行环境奖励信号昂贵、缓慢或部署时不可用。然而现有评判器要么手工编写评分标准（如G-Eval），要么微调评判器权重，两者都倾向于把流畅但失败的轨迹误判为成功。需要一个可信任的、无奖励信号的评判器。

Method: RubricForge方法包含：（1）从少量带真实环境奖励标签的轨迹中提取评判标准文本；（2）通过反射式进化（reflective evolution）迭代地改进评判标准，使其与真实奖励标签的吻合度最大化；（3）冻结最终优化后的评判标准，对留出轨迹进行一次模型调用完成评判，无需访问环境。优化的产物是人类可读的文本，每条评判都能追溯到具体命名准则。使用同一个冻结的7B模型同时作为agent和judge。

Result: 在tau-bench（220次rollout中抽取173条标注轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上，主要收获是忠实度（faithfulness）而非总体一致性。相比泛用G-Eval评判器，McNemar p=0.248表明总体一致性的优势不显著；绝对分数校准上泛用评判器略优（|err|差异-0.048，p=2×10^-4）。但RubricForge将失败轨迹误判为成功的频率约为后者一半（tau-bench上false-pass率0.115 vs 0.173，三次过信捕获、零次反转），且在WebShop排名上更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于无奖励信号的评判器，部署相关的关键指标是false-pass率而非总体一致性，因为误判成功会导致残缺agent被发布上线，而误判失败只是多花一次重试成本。RubricForge在降低false-pass率上显著优于G-Eval类方法，并且其人类可读的评判标准为每次评判结果提供了可归因性。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [91] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）进行了系统性的逐层敏感性分析，发现层敏感性强烈依赖深度：早期和中间层对专家掩码高度脆弱，而晚期层（尤其35-39层）可容忍激进的低幅值专家掩码。在500-prompt验证集上，仅掩码640/10240个专家的窄幅晚期策略可保留419/500个Good+Similar输出，优于30%全层掩码的150/300。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽被广泛采用，但各MoE层的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画，尤其在模型压缩场景下。作者旨在通过系统的层敏感性分析，为深度感知的MoE专家掩码提供经验基础，推动更有效的模型压缩。

Method: 在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上，使用基于幅值的专家掩码方法对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型执行多阶段研究，涵盖100/300/500 prompt评价尺度，跨3台H100 GPU服务器。实验涵盖全层平坦掩码、晚期层聚焦策略、极晚期窄策略等不同掩码策略，并额外探索了top-k路由宽度从8减至6的效果。

Result: 核心发现是层敏感性呈强深度依赖性：早期层（0-9）和中间层（10-29）对专家掩码高度脆弱；晚期层（30-39）尤其极晚期层（35-39）可容忍激进掩码。30%全层掩码仅保留150/300 Good+Similar输出，而晚期策略在掩码640-1145个专家时保留249-255/300。500-prompt验证中，极晚期策略（35-39层@50%）保留419/500输出且仅掩码640个专家。top-k从8降至6在100-prompt探针上显著降低墙钟时间且无Good+Similar损失，但与激进专家掩码尚不能干净组合。

Conclusion: 层敏感性深度依赖性为深度感知的MoE专家掩码提供了实证基础，并指明了一条通向物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分和训练后恢复的实用路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [92] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析（N=46个任务，涵盖语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理四个认知领域），发现大语言模型（LLMs）会发展出与人类大脑相似的模块化架构：在人类中调用同一认知网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而调用不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元。这表明模块化组织可能是智能系统的普遍属性，而非生物大脑特有的进化偶然事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑表现出显著的功能特化模块化组织（如语言、形式推理、心理推理、物理推理等不同网络）。核心问题是：这种模块化组织是构建智能系统的根本原则，还是生物大脑进化过程中产生的偶然产物？作者试图通过在LLM这一通过完全不同的优化过程产生的智能系统中检验是否会出现类似的组织结构来回答这一问题。

Method: 作者使用电路分析（circuit analyses）方法，对N=46个任务进行系统分析，这些任务横跨四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）。关键在于比较：在人类中共享同一认知网络的任务与在LLM中是否招募重叠的神经元；以及在人类中分属不同网络的任务是否在LLM中招募不同的神经元。通过这种跨系统的映射比较来检验模块化结构是否趋同涌现。

Result: 研究发现LLM发展出与人类大脑镜像对应的模块化架构：人类中同一认知网络支持的任务在LLM中由重叠的神经元承担，而人类中不同认知网络的任务在LLM中则由截然不同的神经元承担。即LLM中表现出了跨领域的功能模块化组织。

Conclusion: 大脑和神经网络中模块化的趋同涌现表明，模块化组织可能是智能系统的基本属性，而非生物进化中的偶然事件。这一发现对理解智能系统的普适设计原则具有重要意义。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [93] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于Chutes平台一年的生产级LLM服务追踪数据，从全局表征和纵向研究两个维度深入分析真实世界的LLM服务工作负载模式，并将完整的一年追踪数据随论文发布，以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务工作负载研究在规模和时间跨度上存在局限，通常只观察短时间窗口，无法充分捕捉工作负载随时间的演化以及用户与模型交互对生产流量形态的影响，因此需要更大规模、更长周期的真实生产数据来更好地激励和基准测试LLM服务系统。

Method: 从Chutes平台收集为期一年的生产级LLM服务追踪数据，该数据覆盖多模型、多用户的完整生产行为（包括热门模型和长尾模型）。从聚合层面、时间维度、模型层面和用户层面四个角度对工作负载进行分析，揭示通常被聚合视图掩盖的工作负载演化和用户-模型结构。

Result: 通过多维度分析揭示了真实LLM服务工作负载的演化规律和用户-模型交互结构，这些模式在传统聚合视图中不可见。完整的年度追踪数据将随论文公开发布，为后续研究提供不依赖采样或合成数据的生产级基准。

Conclusion: 这项研究显著扩展了LLM服务工作负载理解的深度和广度，填补了现有研究在时间跨度和生产可见性方面的空白，并通过公开完整一年的真实追踪数据为LLM服务系统的后续研究提供了宝贵资源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [94] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentao，一个受治理的本地优先运行时系统，用于工具调用型LLM代理。它通过分层架构将模型生成的行动提议与宿主授权的执行分离，使权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹成为显式的运行时抽象，从而构建更可治理、可检查、适合宿主控制的本地环境的代理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为执行系统时具备工具调用、状态修改、持久记忆和外部协议交互等能力，这些能力带来了过度权限操作、弱可审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不受控制的副作用等安全风险。现有系统缺乏对这些风险的系统性治理机制，需要一种将授权与执行分离的运行时架构。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，包括宿主面对接口层、宿主契约层、运行时核心层、权限中介的工具系统层，以及支持记忆、重放、插件、技能、子代理和协议集成等子系统。核心设计思路是将模型生成的行动提议与宿主授权的执行分离，通过显式的权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹等运行时抽象实现治理。

Result: 论文描述了Agentao的动机、威胁模型、设计目标、治理模型、执行流水线和结构化事件接口。系统不提供形式化的安全保证，但展示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹作为显式的运行时抽象，支撑构建更可治理和可检查的代理系统。代码已公开在GitHub上。

Conclusion: Agentao证明了一种基于本地优先运行时的代理治理方法，通过显式化运行时抽象来增强代理系统的可治理性和可检查性，适用于宿主控制的本地环境，但明确不承诺形式化的安全保证。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [95] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张AI评估应从关注超人类自主表现（隐含"替代人类"目标）转向评估人机协作团队的整体绩效，以引导AI发展朝着真正补充人类能力的方向前进，从而带来更好的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估的主流范式以超人类自主表现为核心指标，隐含地将"替代人类"作为AI发展的目标，这种评估导向正在将AI发展引向错误方向。作者认为需要从根本上转变评估视角。

Method: 作为立场论文，本文不提出具体技术方法，而是通过论证分析提出评估范式转变的主张：将评估焦点从单一AI系统的自主表现转向人类-AI团队的协作绩效。

Result: 论文主张这一协作导向的评估转变将促使AI系统真正成为人类能力的互补物，从而比当前基于替代逻辑的评估流程产生更好的社会结果。

Conclusion: AI评估范式应当从以"替代人类"为导向的自主能力评估，转向以"人机协作"为导向的团队绩效评估，这是引导AI技术造福社会的关键性转变。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [96] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中"稳定的错误校准"现象，即高置信度错误答案在微扰下仍保持局部稳定，而非传统认为的脆弱内部推理所致。通过输出级审计评分和内部敏感度探针两种诊断手段，发现自我批评式提示能降低隐藏层敏感度（提示诱导局部稳定化），但审计识别出的过度自信错误并不比自信正确答案更敏感，说明部分高置信度错误是稳定且校准不良的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的高置信度错误常被视为内部推理脆弱的证据，但作者提出另一种可能：存在"稳定的错误校准"——即自信的错误答案在微小扰动下依然局部稳定。这一可能性挑战了将高置信度错误简单归因于推理脆弱性的传统观点，需要用新诊断方法来区分这两种不同机制。

Method: 结合两种诊断方法：(1) 标签感知的输出级审计评分，在强制回答基线下按置信度变化和过度自信错误对领域排序；(2) 内部敏感度探针，测量隐藏状态在扰动下的移动量。在多领域二元事实审计集上，以三个开源权重模型为对象，比较自我批评式提示前后的决策损失与隐含层敏感度。

Result: 审计评分能追踪自我批评式提示对决策损失的改善（但直接标签基线排序更强）；自我批评式提示一致降低三层隐藏状态的敏感度；然而，审计定义的过度自信错误并不比自信正确回答更局部敏感，表明部分高置信度错误是稳定且校准不良的。

Conclusion: 证据支持自我批评式提示诱发了局部稳定化（而非单纯输出层面的弃权模式），但稳定化不等于校准改善。高置信度错误中确实存在一类"稳定且误校准"的情况，而非所有过度自信错误都是推理脆弱的体现。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [97] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性度量（BCM），用于量化智能体的行为一致性，区别于传统结果指标（如成功率）。BCM通过训练模型从智能体执行轨迹中预测任务成功率、提取逐轨迹特征归因向量，并计算智能体系统内这些向量之间的平均两两相似度。基于六个语言模型智能体在软件工程任务上的约9000条轨迹，发现跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是两个可分离的轴，且一致性无法还原为成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估几乎完全依赖成功率和任务完成度等结果指标，这些指标只能反映是否成功，无法反映行为的一致性与稳定性。作者认为行为一致性是一个独立且可测的性质，现有工作仅关注同任务可复现性，无法观察到全局层面的一致性分离现象，因此需要一个新的过程级可靠性信号。

Method: BCM通过三步实现：1）训练一个模型，从智能体的执行轨迹行为特征中预测任务成功与否；2）为每条轨迹导出特征归因向量（feature-attribution vector）；3）计算给定智能体系统内所有轨迹归因向量的平均两两相似度，作为一致性度量。实验覆盖六个语言模型智能体、约9000条软件工程任务轨迹，并通过控制任务难度进行鲁棒性验证。

Result: 研究发现：1）跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是可分离的轴——某些系统在单任务多次尝试中表现出局部可复现性，但在不同任务间呈现全局碎片化，缺乏稳定策略；2）一致性不能还原为成功率，成功率相近的系统一致性可能差异显著；3）在控制任务难度的情况下，前沿模型与开源模型之间的一致性差距依然存在。

Conclusion: BCM作为过程级可靠性信号，可以补充传统结果指标，提供对智能体行为一致性的独立度量。作者同时明确界定了BCM适用的条件与意义边界。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [98] [SemPlan: Benchmarking Structured Semantic Planning for LLM-Based Queries over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13612)
*Bruno Santos Teixeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemPlan Benchmark是一个包含1,800个中英葡双语（英文和巴西葡萄牙语）确定性合成用例的基准，用于评估企业数据自然语言接口的架构设计空间。四种架构（直接SQL生成A1、有界工具Agent A2、结构化语义请求生成+确定性规划执行A3、澄清/有状态语义规划A4）在同一模型配置下对比，结果整体正确率偏低（22%-26%），A3正确率最高且显著优于其他架构，但各架构在不同指标上各有所长，支持权衡而非普适排名的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据自然语言接口需要将模糊的用户请求转化为受治理、可执行的行为，同时控制无效查询、策略失败、成本和不确定性。现有研究缺乏一个确定性、可用双语评估的基准来系统比较不同架构设计空间中的权衡。

Method: 构建SemPlan确定性合成双语基准（1,800个用例，其中1,200个为冻结的科学评估子集），在相同模型配置下对比四种架构：A1直接SQL生成、A2有界工具Agent、A3结构化语义请求生成后进行确定性规划与执行、A4带澄清机制的动态有状态语义规划变体。评估4,800条主记录，涵盖答案正确性、策略符合率、不安全/无效率、API成本、虚假拒绝率及稳定性等指标。

Result: 总体正确率偏低：A1为22.25%、A2为22.58%、A3为25.67%、A4为24.25%。A3在预设配对分析中显著优于A1、A2和A4，正确率最高；A1保持最高策略符合率和最低不安全/无效率；A4实现最低平均API成本和最低虚假拒绝率。在150个用例的预选稳定性子集上，答案正确可重复率为92.00%-98.67%。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持权衡解释而非普适排序：增加结构约束会改变失败模式和效率，但不会单调提升正确率，也无法完全解决歧义性和多轮状态一致性问题。不同架构在不同维度各有优势，应根据具体需求在设计空间中选择。

Abstract: Natural-language interfaces to enterprise data must translate underspecified requests into governed, executable behavior while controlling invalid queries, policy failures, cost, and nondeterminism. SemPlan Benchmark evaluates this architectural design space with a deterministic synthetic bilingual benchmark containing 1,800 cases in English and Brazilian Portuguese; 1,200 cases form the frozen scientific evaluation subset. Four architectures are compared under the same model configuration: direct SQL generation (A1), a bounded tool-agent baseline (A2), structured semantic-request generation followed by deterministic planning and execution (A3), and a clarification/stateful semantic-plan variant (A4). Across 4,800 primary records, answer correctness was low in absolute terms: 22.25% for A1, 22.58% for A2, 25.67% for A3, and 24.25% for A4. A3 had the highest observed correctness and significantly exceeded A1, A2, and A4 in the pre-specified paired correctness analysis, while A1 retained the highest policy-correct rate and the lowest unsafe-or-invalid rate. A4 had the lowest mean API cost and lowest false-refusal rate. On a preselected 150-case stability subset, answer-correct repeatability ranged from 92.00% to 98.67%. The results support a trade-off interpretation rather than a universal ranking: additional structural constraints changed failure modes and efficiency, but did not monotonically improve correctness or solve ambiguity and multi-turn state consistency.

</details>


### [99] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于误解的分层分析框架，将误解视为一个"分歧产生→分歧放大→检测与修复"的分层过程，识别出11种精确故障模式，映射到通信过程的8个分析层，并形式化建模，将信息与通信理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着沟通从实时面对面交互转向AI中介渠道，沟通者失去了依赖的修复资源，而误解检测的新手段尚未建立，因此误解检测成为亟待解决的问题。现有研究分散在九个领域且互不引用，缺乏统一的分类框架。

Method: 整合九个通常互不引用领域的研究成果，识别出11种精确故障模式；根据每种机制在通信过程中作用的特定点，推导出8个分析层；对8个层进行形式化建模，将信息与通信理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建；提供逐源证据矩阵、编码手册和9个解析的对话案例。

Result: 识别出11种故障模式：8种机制主要产生分歧，2种主要放大已存在的分歧，1种控制分歧是否被检测和修复。提供了可审计的证据矩阵、编码手册和9个对话分析案例，形成了一种同时按过程定位和功能分类的误解分类法。

Conclusion: 本文是首个既能在通信过程特定点定位误解机制、又能按功能类型对其进行分类的误解分类研究，为AI中介沟通环境下的误解检测提供了系统化理论框架。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [100] [Ontology-Grounded Project Memory for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13662)
*James Adam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOOSEDev是一个基于本体和知识图谱的编码代理项目记忆系统，通过MCP接口向代理暴露架构决策、经验教训和约束等结构化记忆，并使用MOOSE神经符号引擎作为推理基底。在835条类型化记录上与生产级向量记忆工具对比，MOOSEDev在取代、集合完整性和否定提问上的答案召回率达0.98-1.00，而基准top-k检索仅6%-27%；在相关性召回率和token成本上两者相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编码代理成为软件项目代码生成的主要手段，代码变更速度加快，追踪变更背后的原因变得困难。传统向量记忆工具缺乏结构化和语义推理能力，难以准确回答涉及记录取代（supersession）、集合完整性（set-completeness）和否定（negation）等复杂语义问题。

Method: 构建一个知识图谱作为项目记忆载体，记录架构决策、经验教训、约束及理由；记录带有生命周期状态、来源和取代链接等元数据。通过Model Context Protocol (MCP)接口向编码代理暴露此记忆。采用MOOSE这一专有神经符号引擎，以符号层作为主要推理基底进行查询。实验在835条类型化记录的公共语料上，与生产级向量记忆工具对比，衡量预期答案集召回率及token成本。

Result: MOOSEDev在取代、集合完整性和否定类问题上的预期答案集召回率达0.98-1.00，而基线向量工具的top-k检索仅召回6%-27%；在相关性召回率（relevance recall）和token成本方面两系统基本相当。此外还完成了自身代码库的临时提交历史引导（temporal commit-history bootstrap）、预先注册的实时试验，并总结了经验教训。

Conclusion: MOOSEDev证明了基于本体和符号推理的项目记忆系统在需要精确语义推理的查询上显著优于纯向量检索，同时未牺牲相关性召回率与token效率。这项工作为编码代理的可追溯记忆管理提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Coding agents have become the primary means of generating new code in many software projects, and the resulting velocity of changes makes keeping track of the reasons behind those changes challenging. This paper introduces MOOSEDev, a system designed to give coding agents structured, ontology-grounded project memory. The system captures architectural decisions, lessons, constraints, and rationales in a knowledge graph exposed to agents via a Model Context Protocol (MCP) interface. Records carry lifecycle status, provenance, and supersession links, queryable via MOOSE, a proprietary neurosymbolic engine that treats the symbolic layer as the primary reasoning substrate. We compared MOOSEDev against a production vector-memory tool on a neutral public corpus of 835 typed records. MOOSEDev returned the expected answer set essentially in full (0.98-1.00) on supersession, set-completeness, and negation questions, whereas the baseline's top-k retrieval surfaced between 6% and 27%. Conversely, relevance recall and token cost were largely equivalent between the two systems. We also describe a temporal commit-history bootstrap of our own codebase, a pre-registered live trial, and lessons learned.

</details>


### [101] [Content Depth Matters in Short-Video Recommendation: Rethinking the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13990)
*Liwei Deng,Jing Jiang,Zhiwei Li,Yang Wang,Guodong Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出内容深度评分（CDS）指标来衡量短视频刺激高阶认知过程的程度，并构建了首个用于短视频推荐内容深度评估的基准SCOPE-Bench，包含150K视频的CDS标注。通过评估13个代表性推荐系统，发现现有RSs普遍偏向浅层内容，且其对认知深度内容的推荐效果仅略优于随机选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频推荐系统在注意力经济驱动下过度优化用户参与度，导致系统天然偏好能瞬间吸引注意力的浅层内容。但已有证据表明长期接触此类内容可能损害用户的认知参与和心理健康，引发对社会长期影响的担忧。现有推荐目标忽视了内容深度这一维度，缺乏系统性量化与评估工具。

Method: 1) 提出内容深度评分（CDS），基于认知心理学和学习理论建立七级量表，衡量视频预期刺激高阶认知过程的程度；2) 构建SCOPE-Bench基准，基于大规模开源短视频数据集，为150K视频提供CDS标注；3) 利用该基准对13个代表性推荐系统进行系统性评估。

Result: 评估发现所有13个代表性推荐系统均一致性地偏向推荐浅层内容；算法在推荐认知深度内容方面的表现仅略优于随机选择，揭示出现有推荐目标中此前被忽视的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有的短视频推荐系统在内容深度维度上存在系统性缺陷，需要重新审视推荐优化目标，纳入认知内容深度考量，以促进更健康、更具认知价值的短视频生态。

Abstract: Driven by the attention economy, short-video Recommender Systems (RSs) are primarily optimized to maximize user engagement by promoting videos that capture attention within seconds. These systems inherently favor shallow-content videos that are effective at attracting immediate attention. However, growing evidence suggests that prolonged exposure to such content may negatively affect users' cognitive engagement and mental well-being, raising concerns about the long-term societal impact of the short-video platform. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces a new metric, the \textbf{Content Depth Score (CDS)}, to quantify the content depth of short videos. CDS measures the extent to which a video is expected to stimulate higher-order cognitive processes, using a seven-level scale grounded in established theories of cognitive psychology and learning. As an initial step toward this vision, we present \textbf{SCOPE-Bench}, the first benchmark for content-depth evaluation in short-video recommendation. Built upon a large-scale open-source short-video dataset, SCOPE-Bench provides CDS annotations for 150K videos, enabling systematic evaluation of RSs from a cognitive-content perspective. Leveraging SCOPE-Bench, we evaluate 13 representative RSs and reveal a consistent preference for shallow-content videos. Moreover, we find that these algorithms recommending cognitively deep content are only marginally better than random selection, highlighting a previously overlooked limitation of existing recommendation objectives. Our code and datasets are available at https://liweidengdavid.github.io/SCOPE-Bench/.

</details>


### [102] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个主动感知框架用于具身环境中的目标消歧，以主动观察为核心信息获取手段，由视觉-语言模型决定何时继续观察、请求澄清或完成目标选择，并在真实机器人实验中验证了其统一物理信息获取与用户意图澄清的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身环境中的目标歧义不仅来源于用户意图不清，还可能因当前观测中缺少与任务相关的物理证据（如遮挡、视角受限、文本不可读、目标未被观察到）而产生。现有的交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问来获取额外信息，忽略了机器人主动改变观测这一关键途径。

Method: 提出一个以主动观察为信息获取主干的主动感知框架，利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）基于累积的视觉证据和交互信息做出决策，判断应采取三种动作之一：继续观察、请求用户澄清或完成目标选择。主动观察既能直接恢复缺失的判别性物理证据，也能揭示物体名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍需澄清时提升用户澄清的效率。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一在同一个具身消歧流程中，取得了有效的目标消歧性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明将主动感知与视觉-语言模型决策相结合，可以在一个统一的框架内同时处理物理证据缺失和用户意图歧义这两类目标歧义来源，为具身机器人交互中的目标识别提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [103] [Second Thought: Reasoning in Parallel as LLM Agents Act and Observe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13667)
*Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Yunbo Lyu,Jieke Shi,David Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Second Thought是一种免训练的推理框架，通过在每个Thought阶段结束时派生四个辅助分支进行并行解码，将额外推理从主线程的串行解码路径中移出，从而利用行动与观察之间的推理空闲窗口，在不牺牲准确率的情况下降低智能体的回合数和主线程解码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ReAct范式的LLM智能体中，推理仅限于Thought阶段，而在Action和Observation的执行等待期间智能体的推理处于空闲状态。作者识别出这个"推理空闲窗口"（reasoning idle window），并思考能否利用它进行并行推理以服务未来的轮次，从而提升整体效率。

Method: Second Thought提出了一种免训练的推理框架：在每个Thought阶段结束时立即派生出四个辅助分支，它们与主循环并发解码；当环境观测到达时，将辅助分支生成的思考内容合并回主线程。通过这种方式，额外的推理被移出主线程的串行解码路径，实现了推理的并行化。

Result: 在三个智能体基准和三个推理LLM上，Second Thought在所有九组（模型，基准）中均降低了平均回合数，并在其中六组将主线程解码量减少了最多43%（这些设置中平均约20%），第七组基本不变；Pass@1在九组中有七组无显著变化，两组显著差异分别为+12.4和+10.2分。与计算量匹配的对照组相比，在四种适用设置中，Pass@1严格更高且串行解码减少1.3到3.2倍。

Conclusion: Second Thought验证了ReAct范式中存在可利用的推理空闲窗口，通过并行辅助分支将额外推理从主线程串行路径中剥离，在不损失（甚至提升）任务准确率的前提下显著减少了回合数和主线程解码开销，为免训练情况下优化LLM agent推理效率提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM agents in the ReAct paradigm alternate between reasoning, acting, and observing, but deliberate reasoning is confined to the Thought phase: while the agent serializes an action and waits for the environment, its reasoning is frozen. We identify this recurring interval for Action and Observation as a reasoning idle window and ask whether it can host additional reasoning in parallel that serves future turns. Therefore, we propose Second Thought, a training-free inference framework that forks four auxiliary branches the instant each Thought phase concludes, decodes them concurrently with the main loop, and merges the generated thoughts back when the environment observation arrives. In this way, Second Thought relocates the added reasoning off the main thread's sequential decoding path. Across three agentic benchmarks and three reasoning LLMs, Second Thought lowers the average turn count in all nine (model,benchmark) pairs and reduces main thread decoding in six of them by up to 43% (roughly 20% on average among those settings), while leaving it essentially unchanged in a seventh; Pass@1 shows no significant change in seven of nine pairs and the two significant differences are +12.4 and +10.2 points. Against a compute-matched control that forces an equivalent budget onto the main thread's own reasoning, it attains strictly higher Pass@1 with 1.3 to 3.2 less sequential decoding in all four settings where the control applies.

</details>


### [104] [Residual Dominance as a Structural Account of Last-Item Reliance in Causal Self-Attention Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14021)
*Keito Kozaki,Keigo Sakurai,Ren Togo,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对基于Transformer的顺序推荐器中极端依赖于最近交互项的行为提供了结构性解释。通过预测时诊断和基于范数的全注意力块分析，发现SASRec类模型存在显著的"最后一项依赖"现象，根源在于残差连接主导（residual dominance）——残差加法使全块表示偏向同位置贡献。通过推理时残差缩放作为受控诊断干预，验证了残差强度与结构混合/最后项依赖之间的单调权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Transformer顺序推荐器在推理时严重依赖最近交互项是已知现象，但该行为在用于预测的表示中是如何结构性表达的尚不清楚。作者旨在揭示这种依赖的机制性根源，而不仅仅是经验观察。

Method: 结合预测时诊断与基于范数的全注意力块分析。具体包括：1）对SASRec类模型进行最后项依赖的局部性分析；2）通过范数分析揭示残差加法如何使全块表示偏向同位置贡献（residual dominance）；3）推理时残差缩放作为受控诊断干预，探测残差强度与结构混合/最后项依赖之间的权衡。

Result: SASRec类模型表现出高度局部化的最后项依赖；残差加法显著将全块表示推向同位置贡献（残差主导）；改变残差强度会在结构混合与最后项依赖之间产生单调权衡；降低残差强度可以恢复一部分非末位位置已正确排序真实商品但最终位置预测失败的情况。

Conclusion: 提供了将极端最后项依赖与推理时的残差主导联系起来的结构性解释，为理解Transformer顺序推荐器行为提供了新视角，代码已公开。

Abstract: Transformer-based sequential recommenders with causal self-attention often rely heavily on the most recent interaction at inference time, but how this behavior is structurally expressed in the representation used for prediction remains unclear. We combine prediction-time diagnostics with norm-based analysis of the full attention block. First, we show that SASRec-style models exhibit highly localized last-item reliance. We then find that, although self-attention aggregates contextual information, residual addition sharply shifts the full-block representation toward same-position contributions, which we term residual dominance. To probe this interpretation, we use inference-time residual scaling as a controlled diagnostic intervention. Changing the residual strength induces a monotonic trade-off between structural mixing and last-item reliance, while reducing residual strength recovers a subset of final-position misses for which representations at non-final positions already rank the ground-truth item correctly. Our results provide a structural account linking extreme last-item reliance to residual dominance at inference time. The code is publicly available.

</details>


### [105] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem是一个面向设备端长期记忆研究的基准测试与框架，基于年度规模的移动体验数据，通过知识驱动的合成管线构建连贯且时间一致的长期轨迹，支持文本和多模态场景下的多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断，推动AI代理从信息检索走向经验智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新一代AI代理正从回答孤立问题转向持久化的个人助理，需要长期记忆来积累和利用用户特定经验。然而现有基准无法充分反映真实移动场景中经验数据异构、多模态、持续演进且高度个人化的特点，缺乏用于评估设备端长期记忆能力的标准。

Method: MobileMem采用知识驱动的合成管线构建年尺度、时间一致的长期移动体验轨迹，提供互补的文本与多模态设置，覆盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断等任务，使代理能够记忆过去、理解当下并适应未来。

Result: MobileMem建立了基于年度尺度移动体验的基准框架，支持设备端长期记忆研究，能够对不同维度的记忆能力进行系统评估，使移动AI代理具备连续个人学习能力。

Conclusion: MobileMem通过建模经验而非孤立事实，将长期记忆从信息检索层面提升至经验智能层面，为持续个人学习提供了新的研究框架。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [106] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从推理时可获得的信号中预测大语言模型（LLM）更新后的样本级性能回退（即旧模型答对而新模型答错的回归现象），通过统一的附加值测试比较单模型信号（置信度、logit边缘、注意力熵）和跨版本信号（输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表征漂移），在六个基准、三个任务族、六个模型更新对上发现信号有效性高度依赖任务类型，且没有任何信号在所有情形下普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型频繁更新，虽然整体性能在聚合指标上通常提升，但聚合增益掩盖了样本级回退问题——旧模型正确的响应在新模型中可能出错。现有研究缺乏对"如何在新旧版本更新时预测单个样本是否会回退"的系统性分析，且缺少在统一框架下比较各类信号的系统性工作，这对模型部署与回滚决策至关重要。

Method: 作者提出一个统一的"附加值测试"框架，在置信度基线之上隔离每个信号带来的增量预测增益。信号分为两类：(1)单模型信号：模型自身置信度、logit边缘、注意力熵；(2)跨版本信号：输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL散度、表征漂移。实验覆盖六个基准、三个任务族（多项选择题问答、数学推理、代码生成）和六个模型更新对，并构建一个概念验证的选择性回退机制，将高风险样本路由回旧模型。

Result: 研究发现：(1)信号有效性依赖任务类型——置信度在MCQ和简单数学上最强，而似然/KL类信号在更难的数学和代码任务上提供更频繁的增益；(2)没有任何信号在所有模型更新中普遍最优；(3)某些跨版本信号即使在置信度失效时仍保持信息量，且无需标签即可工作，为无监督的选择性回退提供了支撑。

Conclusion: 实践者可根据任务级别的模式来选择特定模型更新应信任的回归预测信号，选择性回退方案（将高风险样本路由回旧模型）是一个有前景且可落地的方向，同时作者公开了代码以促进复现和后续研究。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [107] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标注基准的评测框架，用于评估网络安全场景中"持续学习马具"（LLM配检索或记忆系统的智能体学习系统）的端到端效果。核心思想是基于缩放假设：用更强的教师模型向带持续学习马具的弱学生模型提供稀疏修正，通过学生向教师收敛的速度来评分。实验表明教师相对提升与真实黄金标准提升正相关，可作为无标签时的代理指标，同时验证了LLM-as-a-judge在相似规模模型间无法提供有效信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全运营场景中，传统的基准标签评测方式失效：标签稀缺、过时且不具代表性，实践者无法判断马具系统是否有帮助或哪个更好。LLM-as-a-judge也因不比被评代智能体更强而缺乏区分度，蒸馏在稀疏、断续且有偏标签上不可靠。需要一个无需标注基准即可端到端评估学习马具的框架。

Method: 提出"教师相对提升"（teacher-relative lift）评测框架：更强教师模型向配备持续学习马具的小学生模型提供稀疏、高精度的修正反馈，通过测量学生随时间向教师收敛的程度来量化马具的效果。在多种安全任务、模型家族和马具设计上，将教师相对提升与保留黄金标准上的提升进行相关性验证。

Result: 跨安全任务、模型家族和马具设计中，学生相对教师的提升与其相对保留黄金标准的提升显著正相关，验证了教师相对提升可作无标签场景下真实马具提升的代理指标。同时证明LLM-as-a-judge在功率相近的模型之间不产生可用信号。该结果暗示同一马具在人类提供同类稀疏高精度修正时可用于改进教师规模的模型。

Conclusion: 在没有标注基准的运营安全场景下，教师相对提升是一种有效且可验证的持续学习马具评测代理指标。这一框架不仅解决了标签稀缺时的评测难题，还暗示了马具的通用性——同一机制可扩展到人类修正驱动的教师级模型持续改进。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [108] [How Compliant is Sepsis Treatment? An Expert-Guided Neuro-symbolic Pipeline for Generating Clinical Compliance Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13617)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Kaushik Roy,Subash Neupane,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专家引导的神经-符号混合管线，将大语言模型严格限制于语义归一化任务，并采用Sugeno模糊推理系统对标准化后的临床事件进行循证方案依从性评估。该系统在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症病例上编码了八条Surviving Sepsis Campaign（SSC）束规则，以[0,1]区间的分级分数替代二元判断，揭示了抗生素时机为最关键短板（均值0.24，仅13%在一小时内）、Hour-1表现不佳（均值36.7%）、高乳酸下降率51%等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床护理是否遵循循证方案是一个天然的神经-符号问题，但安全关键场景使纯神经或纯符号范式均难以单独胜任，需要混合方法同时兼顾自然语言理解能力与可解释、可验证的逻辑推理。

Method: 采用专家引导的管线设计：大语言模型仅负责语义归一化（将杂乱的药物和微生物字符串映射到固定临床词汇），Sugeno模糊推理系统负责对归一化事件进行推理，编码八条SSC束规则并以分级分数替代二元判断。

Result: 在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症病例中，抗生素时机是最关键短板（均值0.24，仅13%在一小时内），Hour-1表现不佳（均值36.7%），51%的升乳酸数据存在丢失，且依从性不同组间ICU住院时长有显著差异（3.8天对比5.1天）。

Conclusion: 该混合方法成功将LLM的语义理解能力与模糊逻辑的鲁棒推理相结合，在安全关键临床场景中有效实现了循证方案的自动化依从性评估，并识别出脓毒症护理中的具体薄弱环节。

Abstract: Verifying whether clinical care follows evidence-based protocols is a natural neuro-symbolic problem, yet the safety-critical setting defeats either paradigm alone. We present an expert-guided pipeline that constrains a large language model strictly to semantic normalization, mapping messy drug and microbiology strings onto a fixed clinical vocabulary, while a Sugeno fuzzy inference system reasons over the normalized events. The fuzzy layer encodes eight Surviving Sepsis Campaign bundle rules and replaces binary judgments with graded scores in [0,1]. Applied to 2,438 MIMIC-IV v3.1 sepsis episodes, it surfaces antibiotic timing as the most critical breakdown (mean 0.24, 13% within one hour), Hour-1 underperformance (mean 36.7%), a 51% elevated-lactate drop-off, and descriptive differences in ICU stay across compliance groups (3.8 versus 5.1 days).

</details>


### [109] [Algorithm Design and Physician Liability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13618)
*Shujie Luan,Shubhranshu Singh,Tinglong Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究美国引入的AI算法责任规则如何影响AI公司的算法设计决策与医生的AI使用决策。责任规则可能导致医生减少AI使用，并在中等责任水平下对弱势群体的AI依赖降低；这种效应是非单调的。此外，强制要求算法在不同患者群体间准确率均等反而可能伤害所有群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在临床决策中广泛应用，单一临床算法在不同患者群体间的准确率可能存在差异，引发公平性担忧。美国引入的责任规则使医疗提供者在依赖有偏算法导致错误临床决策时承担责任。作者希望理解这种责任规则如何重塑AI公司的算法设计动机和医生的使用决策，从而评估其政策效果。

Method: 构建一个理论模型：AI公司为两个患者群体设计算法，提升弱势群体准确率的成本更高；医生作为最终问责的决策者，在权衡临床不确定性减少与责任暴露之间决定是否使用AI。通过博弈分析考察责任规则对AI使用、算法设计及群体间差异的均衡影响。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 责任规则可导致AI的不同使用——医生可能整体减少AI使用，且在中间责任区间内对弱势群体更少使用AI；(2) 该效应非单调——随着责任增加，医生对弱势群体的AI使用先降后升，因为公司在高责任下会将投资重新分配以缩小差异或转向等准确率设计；(3) 强制算法群体间准确率均等可能适得其反，因为这会扭曲公司的投资激励和医生的均衡使用决策，最终伤害所有患者群体。

Conclusion: 责任规则对医疗AI公平性的影响是复杂且非单调的。设计不当的监管（如强制等准确率要求）可能产生意想不到的负面后果。政策制定者需要综合考虑AI公司的投资激励与医生的使用决策之间的战略互动，而非简单地施加统一准确性标准。

Abstract: A single clinical algorithm can deliver unequal accuracy across patient groups, and concern about such disparity has grown as artificial intelligence (AI) spreads through clinical decision-making. In response, a liability rule introduced in the United States holds healthcare providers responsible when their reliance on disparate algorithms contributes to erroneous clinical decisions. We examine how such liability considerations reshape (i) an AI firm's algorithm design decisions that drive group-specific accuracy and (ii) a physician's decisions to use AI in healthcare delivery. The AI firm designs an algorithm for two patient groups, and improving accuracy for the disadvantaged group is more costly. The physician (who remains the accountable decision-maker) then decides whether to consult AI, weighing the reduction in clinical uncertainty against expected liability exposure when AI errors disproportionately affect the disadvantaged group. We find the liability rule can induce disparate use of AI: the physician may reduce AI use overall and, over an intermediate range of liability, rely on AI less for disadvantaged patients. The effect is non-monotone. As liability increases, the physician's use of AI for disadvantaged patients first declines, then rises as the firm reallocates investment toward reducing disparity or switches to an equal-accuracy design. Mandating equal algorithmic accuracy across patient groups can then inadvertently harm both groups, because a uniform accuracy requirement distorts the firm's investment incentives and the physician's equilibrium AI-use decisions.

</details>


### [110] [Your Probabilistic JEPA Is Secretly a Hidden Markov Model: A State-Space Interpretation of Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13621)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了时间索引的预测信息瓶颈JEPA（PIB-VJEPA）与隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）之间共享相同的计算结构，并提出马尔可夫链JEPA（MCJEPA）作为具体实例，通过状态转移矩阵实现对HMM的精确序列级等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）在自监督学习中取得了成功，但其缺乏对隐状态推理的严格形式化理解。作者希望为时间JEPA建立有原则的状态空间解释，将其与成熟的HMM框架联系起来，从而为预测学习和隐变量动力学提供理论基础。

Method: 论文建立了一种映射关系：将随机上下文编码器类比为HMM中的摊销滤波分布，将概率预测器类比为隐状态马尔可夫转移，将解码器/逆目标编码器/隐式条件分布类比为发射方向。作者区分了4个逐步增强的对应层次，给出了精确序列级HMM等价的充分条件，并提出了MCJEPA——用可学习的转移矩阵替换隐预测器。在有限时齐情况下，矩阵幂确保多时域的Chapman-Kolmogorov一致性。该构造可推广到条件离散状态转移、连续状态马尔可夫核及连续时间动力学；确定性时间JEPA被解释为退化的Dirac核特例。预测信息瓶颈学习被解读为寻求紧凑的预测状态：压缩促进极小性，残差可预测性检验充分性。

Result: 受控实验支持了转移组合、滤波解释、在已知合成过程中的预测马尔可夫化，以及JEPA隐状态预测与HMM风格序列学习之间的区别。

Conclusion: 这些结果赋予了时间JEPA一个有原则的状态空间解释，桥接了现代自监督表示学习与经典状态空间模型之间的鸿沟，为JEPA类方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: A hidden Markov model (HMM) combines three roles: inference of a hidden-state belief from observations, propagation through a Markov transition, and emission back to observation space. We show that full, time-indexed Predictive Information Bottleneck VJEPA (PIB-VJEPA) exposes the same computational structure: a stochastic context encoder plays the role of an amortized filtering distribution, a probabilistic predictor defines latent-state dynamics, and a decoder, inverse target encoder, or induced implicit conditional supplies the emission direction. We distinguish 4 progressively stronger levels of correspondence and give sufficient conditions for exact sequence-level HMM equivalence. To make the connection concrete, we introduce Markov-Chain JEPA (MCJEPA), which replaces the latent predictor by a learned transition matrix; in the finite time-homogeneous case, matrix powers guarantee exact multi-horizon Chapman--Kolmogorov consistency. Conditioned discrete-state transitions, continuous-state Markov kernels, and continuous-time dynamics extend this construction, while deterministic temporal JEPA appears as a degenerate Dirac-kernel special case. We further interpret predictive information-bottleneck learning as seeking a compact predictive state: compression promotes minimality, while residual predictability tests sufficiency. Controlled experiments support transition composition, the filtering interpretation, predictive Markovization in a known synthetic process, and the distinction between JEPA latent prediction and HMM-style sequence learning. Together, these results give temporal JEPA a principled state-space interpretation.

</details>


### [111] [ARC: Fair Relative Advantage Comparison in Open-Ended Real-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13622)
*Yongqi Tong,Tan Li Hui Faith,Choy Zhen Wen Marcus,Zhou Jin,Kewei Fu,Jiang-Ming Yang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC（通过条件化的优势正则化）训练方法，解决开放式交互中基于组的RL因行为不可比导致的奖励公平性问题，并提出了inter范式及其86K标注数据集，实验显示ARC显著提升工具使用基准，inter将首token延迟从4.91s降至1.27s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式真实交互允许多种有效行为（如直接回答、询问澄清、提供进度更新等），这打破了基于组的RL（group-based RL）的核心假设——组内rollout在行为上不可比。这导致奖励模型对互动风格的偏好会扭曲相对优势，使优化偏向奖励偏好行为而非上下文适当行为。作者将其正式化为奖励公平性问题（reward fairness problem）。

Method: 提出ARC（Advantage Regularization via Conditioning）训练方案：(1) 通过策略条件化的rollout分组恢复公平的相对比较；(2) 结合混合奖励（hybrid rewards）和熵正则化（entropy regularization）。同时提出inter范式，将用户可见通信与潜在推理和工具使用解耦，实现响应式、可引导且感知执行的人机交互，并构建了inter-86K策略标注训练语料及配套的标注和蒸馏pipeline。

Result: ARC显著增强τ/τ²工具使用基准性能；inter将time-to-first-token从4.91s降低到1.27s（相对于think-style基线大幅提升）。ARC实现和inter-86K训练数据将开源发布。

Conclusion: 开放式交互学习的关键瓶颈不仅在于智能体如何被奖励，更在于其行为是否首先被公平比较。通过恢复公平的相对比较机制，可以更有效地训练出上下文适当的行为。

Abstract: Open-ended real-world interaction admits multiple valid behaviors: an agent may answer directly, ask for clarification, provide progress updates, or confirm before acting. This flexibility breaks a core assumption behind group-based RL: rollouts compared within a group are no longer guaranteed to be behaviorally comparable. As a result, reward-model preferences over interaction style can distort relative advantages and steer optimization toward reward-preferred behaviors rather than context-appropriate ones. We formalize this as a \textit{reward fairness problem} and propose \textbf{ARC} (Advantage Regularization via Conditioning), a training recipe that restores fairer relative comparison through strategy-conditioned rollout grouping, together with hybrid rewards and entropy regularization. We study ARC in our proposed \inter, a novel paradigm for responsive, steerable, and execution-aware user-agent interaction that decouples user-visible communication from latent reasoning and tool use. \inter\ also provides the annotation and distillation pipeline for constructing \inter-86K, our strategy-annotated training corpus for supervised and RL training. Empirically, ARC substantially strengthens the core $τ/τ^2$ tool-use benchmarks, while \inter\ reduces time-to-first-token from 4.91s to 1.27s relative to a think-style baseline. Together, these results suggest that a central bottleneck in open-ended interactive learning is not only how agents are rewarded, but whether their behaviors are compared fairly in the first place. The ARC implementation and \inter-86K training data will be released.

</details>


### [112] [Reward Machines for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13625)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Shangtong Zhang,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自动机的STL（信号时序逻辑）控制综合方法，通过构造定时交替自动机为强化学习提供高效记忆机制和马尔可夫奖励，解决了传统STL鲁棒性奖励因依赖执行历史而导致的状态空间爆炸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL规范被广泛用于指定实时属性，但其鲁棒性分数依赖执行历史，导致在长期、任意嵌套时序操作符的规范下，将其作为强化学习奖励会引发难以处理的状态空间扩展。此外，现代自主和AI系统缺乏精确完整的系统模型，优化方法不适用，需要学习驱动的控制策略。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于自动机的方法：(1) 从STL规范构造定时交替自动机(timed alternating automaton)；(2) 用自动机位置和时钟估值扩展状态空间作为记忆机制；(3) 从自动机接受条件推导马尔可夫奖励，使奖励不再依赖完整执行历史，适合强化学习框架。

Result: 实验表明，该方法学习到的策略在鲁棒性分数和满足率方面均优于使用鲁棒性奖励的现有方法。

Conclusion: 基于自动机的记忆机制和马尔可夫奖励有效解决了STL强化学习中的状态空间爆炸问题，在复杂长时域规范下能学习到更优的控制策略。

Abstract: Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for specifying real-time properties of real-valued observations, along with a quantitative robustness score for monitoring satisfaction. Control synthesis from STL specifications is of interest since manual controller design becomes infeasible as real-world systems grow in complexity. Moreover, many modern autonomous and AI-enabled systems lack accurate and complete system models, which makes optimization-based synthesis approaches unsuitable and motivates learning-based control. Prior work uses STL robustness scores as rewards in reinforcement learning (RL) to obtain control policies satisfying given specifications; however, robustness depends on execution history, leading to intractable state space expansion for general long-horizon specifications with arbitrarily nested temporal operators. This work introduces a novel automata-based approach that provides an efficient memory mechanism and associated Markovian rewards suitable for RL frameworks. Our approach constructs a timed alternating automaton from the given STL specifications, augments the state space with automaton locations and clock valuations, and derives rewards from the automaton acceptance condition. We empirically demonstrate that our approach learns policies that achieve higher robustness scores and satisfaction rates than those learned by existing approaches using robustness-based rewards.

</details>


### [113] [A Calibrated Test of Internal Action Maps: State Signals Without Global Affine Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13626)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个证据格（evidence lattice）系统检验"隐藏状态信号可解码/可因果使用"与"存在可复用的动作映射"之间的可分离性。在仿射S_5载体上，所有保留源的折叠均通过一步、合成、逆、解码和交换测试；但结构化曲率和保持域共轭会单调增加误差，仅23/30最强单元翻转闭合门，表明校准有边界而非普适。在Qwen/Qwen3-4B上，冻结末token h28仿射映射的保留实体误差为.519（域内交叉拟合为.398），七个随机实体划分不支持纯实体特异性解释。因果干预仅出现在h28/h36，重构虽改善一步误差但无重构通过合成门。结论是状态可用性、因果使用、局部几何和可复用闭合在测试载体上可分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
